It does NOT re-scan: a task spawned DURING the drain is outside the one-shot snapshot. **Start command, byte-identical in `railway.json` and `Procfile`:** `exec uvicorn ... --timeout-graceful-shutdown 20 --limit-concurrency "${UVICORN_LIMIT_CONCURRENCY:-512}"`, plus `deploy.drainingSeconds: 30`. The load-bearing inequality is `drainingSeconds (30) > graceful (20) + DRAIN_TIMEOUT (8)`, because uvicorn's `--timeout-graceful-shutdown` bounds ONLY its own request-task wait and lifespan shutdown (where the drain runs) happens AFTER it, outside that timeout — the installed default is `None` = unbounded, so before this one hung handler blocked shutdown until Railway SIGKILLed the container. **On-deploy verification still OWED:** the OLD deployment's log must show `[DRAIN]` lines between `Waiting for application shutdown.` and `Application shutdown complete.`, and the deployment details page should show the draining setting as config-file-sourced; if Railway does not honour the key the window silently reverts to the platform default (fallbacks: the dashboard's Deployment teardown → Draining time, or `RAILWAY_DEPLOYMENT_DRAINING_SECONDS`). Config-as-code is deprecated until **2026-12-01** — the IaC migration must carry `drainingSeconds`. Honest limit: this drains a GRACEFUL shutdown only; SIGKILL/OOM drain nothing, and uvicorn skips lifespan shutdown entirely on `force_exit` (a second signal).

**SESSION 66 flags (2026-09-23/24; every row default OFF, read PER CALL via `os.getenv`, NOTHING flipped, Railway NOT read — prod state is inferred from "the names are new"; merged #166 W3-3 → #171 W3-6 → #172 W3-16 → #173 W4-1; #177 W4-3 and #179 W4-4 are OPEN PRs at the close and their rows are written here in advance).** The unflagged units of the session (W3-4, W3-7, W3-9, W3-11bcd, W3-13, W3-14, W3-15, W4-10, and the open W4-9 #178) are in the SESSION 66 Active-runtime block below.
- `ENABLE_SOCIAL_DEVICE_FINGERPRINT` (W3-3, `app/api/auth_routes.py` social-login route, PR #166) — **Effect ON:** the `X-Device-Fingerprint` header a social sign-in (Google/Apple) carries is applied to the created/linked account exactly as the email register path applies it (the referral lifetime-cap and re-signup inheritance then cover social accounts too). A failed apply is SILENT and logged by exception TYPE only (never `str(exc)` — an auth-path SDK exception can carry a credential as its argument). **Flag OFF:** the route's insert and response are byte-identical (pinned). No client change: every client build since 97b5f15 already sends the header.
- `ENABLE_PASSWORD_RESET_DEEP_LINK` (W3-6, `app/services/auth_service.py::password_reset_deep_link_enabled`, PR #171) + optional override `PASSWORD_RESET_REDIRECT_URL` — **Effect ON:** `request_password_reset` passes `{"redirect_to": PASSWORD_RESET_REDIRECT_URL or "qaren://reset-password"}` to `reset_password_email`, so the recovery email returns to the app, where the OTA'd client routes `reset-password` to `Auth > ResetPassword` and completes the reset through the new unauthenticated `POST /api/v1/auth/password-recovery` (5/min; the access token IS the credential; blacklist → upstream `get_user` → fail-closed AMR `recovery` gate → the same admin write `change_user_password` makes → token blacklisted; every failure a 400 with a top-level `code`, never 401). **Flag OFF:** today's exact one-positional `reset_password_email(email)` call (pinned). **HARD PRECONDITIONS:** (1) `qaren://reset-password` on the Supabase project's Redirect-URL allow-list (GoTrue silently falls back to the Site URL otherwise); (2) the W3-6 client OTA on phones; (3) read the first canary's `[auth] password recovery rejected: amr=` line — the AMR claim shape of a recovery session is unmeasured live. A GoTrue 500/429 during `get_user` still reads as a dead link (same gap as W1-4's classifier; follow-up `PO-AUTH-RESET-01`).
- `ENABLE_CONSENT_PERSIST` (W3-16, `app/services/consent_service.py::consent_persist_enabled`, PR #172) — **Effect ON:** `register_user` and the new-account branch of `sign_in_with_social` add `terms_accepted_at` / `terms_version` / `age_attested_at` to the users insert, from the three optional request fields `terms_accepted` / `terms_version` / `age_attested` (`terms_version` recorded AS SENT — what the user saw — never coerced to the server's `TERMS_VERSION`). **Flag OFF:** the insert dicts are byte-identical (`{id,email,subscription_tier}` / `{id,email,auth_provider,subscription_tier}`, pinned). **HARD PRECONDITION:** migration 038 (`migrations/038_users_consent_capture.sql`; applied 2026-09-24, session 67) applied first — without it the insert fails on an unknown column.
- `ENABLE_CONSENT_REQUIRED` (W3-16, `consent_service.consent_required_enabled`, independent of PERSIST) — **Effect ON:** `/api/v1/auth/register` returns 400 `{success:false, code:TERMS_ACCEPTANCE_REQUIRED}` BEFORE any Supabase account is created, and `/social-login` returns the same 400 for a NEW account only (no users row yet; existing accounts are never gated), whenever the request lacks `terms_accepted=true` + `age_attested=true` + a non-empty `terms_version`. **PRECONDITIONS, in order:** (a) the W3-16 client OTA on phones (97b5f15 sends no consent, so every registration from it would be refused); (b) `scripts/bundle_d_prod_smoke.py` probes 5, 10 and 11 post bare `/register` and `/social-login` bodies and turn red — add the three consent fields or announce them expected-red first; (c) flip PERSIST first (REQUIRED without PERSIST rejects but records nothing). **Canary:** the count of `TERMS_ACCEPTANCE_REQUIRED` 400s on the two routes; any non-zero rate means phones without the OTA. **Terms-version bump** (after the legal redraft): TWELVE anchors move together since U8 (the eight below plus the two AR markdown dates `app/legal/{privacy_policy,terms_of_service}_ar.md:5` and the two AR landing dates; the U8 fill-in moves them) — `app/api/legal_routes.py:30` and `:47`, `app/legal/terms_of_service.md:5`, `app/legal/privacy_policy.md:5`, `landing/terms.html:184`, `landing/privacy.html:195`, `app/services/consent_service.py:31`, `SmartCompareApp/src/services/consent.ts:12` (B12 pins three of them; follow-up `PO-CONSENT-01` widens it).
- `ENABLE_SHOPPING_CURRENCY_TRUTH` (W4-1, `app/services/price_service.py::shopping_currency_truth_enabled`, both doors: `extract_price_from_shopping` and the M13-10 stash `_seed_shortcircuit_candidates`, PR #173) — **Effect ON:** (1) the Serper-shopping rung resolves the GCC display token / ISO residue `detect_currency` cannot see BEFORE the strict check and the parse ("22.500 BD" → BHD → 22.5, not 22,500); (2) a known non-target ISO code the effective rate table cannot convert ("TRY 1.299,00" with `ENABLE_EXTENDED_FALLBACK_RATES` off) PENDS instead of shipping the raw amount stamped with the ask currency; (3) `local_bhd` requires HOST evidence: on every ask a missing link, a listing link or any `google.com` host relabels to `converted_usd`; on a BHD ask only, so does a global-tier host, an other-country token in any subdomain label or any path segment, a bahrain-tier registry row that declares `locale_paths` on its bare host, or an off-registry host with no BH marker — amount/url/retailer unchanged. **Flag OFF:** byte-identical on both doors (pinned; the corpus byte-identity harness is blind to this tier by construction). **Composition:** runs BEFORE `ENABLE_SHOPPING_STRICT_CURRENCY`'s pend, deliberately. **Stated limits (pinned):** compound strings ("From 22.500 BD"), Latin Omani "RO", bare numerals, Google ccTLDs (`google.com.bh`, `google.com.sa`), out-of-vocabulary country tokens (`/pk/`). **Canary:** the `[SHOPPING_CURRENCY_TRUTH] relabel … reason=` and `pend unconvertible` INFO lines; watch the genuine-vs-Tier-3 share. Product call for Ahmed: whether the client shows a "(converted from USD)" caption on relabelled rows. **Activation order for the shopping tier:** W4-1 first, then W4-2 (`ENABLE_SHOPPING_DISCOVERY_URL_SPLIT`, red gated, green not built), then W4-3; `ENABLE_REGION_CURRENCY_GUARD` stays LAST.
- `ENABLE_PRESCORING_SHOWABLE_GUARD` (W4-3, `PO-RECORDED-MEASURED-04`, `app/services/price_service.py::prescoring_showable_guard_enabled` + `apply_prescoring_showable_guard`, **OPEN PR #177 at the close**) — mirrors #113: **Effect ON:** every price the response chokepoint would refuse to SHOW (`is_price_showable(..., enforce_correctness=True)`) is pended IN PLACE in both orchestrator twins AFTER `reconcile_pair_fairness` and BEFORE `apply_region_currency_guard`, the `specs`/`prices` yields and `compute_scores`, so `winner_index`, `dimension_winners`, `scoring_v2`, `overview.confidence`, `value_badge` and the verdict input read the same price the user sees; the reason is stashed on the PRODUCT dict as `_prescoring_showable_rejected` (fallback `not_showable`) and harvested into `metadata.guard_rejected`; the chokepoint stays as the idempotent backstop. **Result fork on the phones (why the flag exists):** `[84,72,0,12]`→`[82,70,0,12]`, `[84,76,0,8]`→`[76,81,1,5]` (crown flips) on the unit's fixtures; every pended product takes the -4 price-authority delta; a manufactured cross-tier when both raw prices share a non-mid tier (follow-up `PO-RECORDED-MEASURED-04b` lands BEFORE the flip); `overview.confidence` and `value_badge` also move (a pended product can carry `fair_price`). **Flag OFF:** pure no-op (the helper returns False first; pinned 10a/10b/10c/18 + Preserve 156 in both states; the corpus harness is BLIND to this unit). Needs `ENABLE_EXACT_PRICE_GATE` ON (the prod default). Canary = `scoring_v2.overall_score`/`winner_idx` + the SSE `scores` event + `overview.confidence.overall` + `value_badge`, split by `metadata.guard_rejected` reason; never the final payload alone.
- `ENABLE_HONEST_PARTIAL_SCORING` (W4-4, `PO-VERDICT-TRUTH-02`, `app/services/response_builder.py::_honest_partial_scoring_enabled`, **OPEN PR #179 at the close**) — **Effect ON, SCOPED to partials (`metadata.partial is True`):** a post-gather partial (product data stashed, no scoring) runs `compute_scores` on the stash (ctx `user_preferences` only, no behaviour/cohort profile, no LLM, no network) and ships real scores and the real crown; an early-buffer partial, or one whose scoring still lacks an `overall`, ships `scoring_v2: null` (key kept) with `overview.winner.reason`, `key_tradeoff`, `recommendation` and the persisted BC `comparison` aliases blanked — instead of today's fabricated 70/69 crowned by input order. A compute failure logs ONE WARNING `[W4-4] partial compute_scores failed: <ExceptionType>` and ships the honest shape. A non-partial build with an absent overall keeps today's calibrated block. **Flag OFF:** byte-identical (204-file comm set, empty failure sets). **UNFLAGGED and additive alongside it:** `metadata.partial_stage` ∈ {`gather`, `post_gather`, `scoring`, `verdict`} — the stage the partial CARRIED (W4-13 adopts the mapping). **Precondition:** do NOT flip together with `ENABLE_FULL_STREAM_DEADLINE` until the SSE stash lands — the streaming path never stashes, so a tail-deadline partial with both flags ON RETRACTS scores and a verdict the stream already emitted (latent while phones use REST). Flip AFTER the FE copy row (`results.partial.note`) is on phones so an early partial reads as honest, not empty. Rows persisted before the flip keep 70/69 (backfill predicate `metadata.partial = true AND scoring.scores == {}`).
- **CORRECTION to the `ENABLE_DEFAULT_RATE_LIMITS` row above (measured by the R-MAIN retro red on the PINNED stack, fastapi 0.141.1 / starlette 1.6.0 / slowapi 0.1.10):** the "21 previously-undecorated routes" do NOT meet the blanket 10/min default. `app.routes` holds 14 `_IncludedRouter` entries without `.endpoint`, so slowapi's `_find_route_handler` returns None for every router-included path and `_should_exempt(None)` is True in BOTH `SlowAPIMiddleware` and `SlowAPIASGIMiddleware`; only the 3 app-level routes (`/health`, `/`, `/favicon.ico`) are ever default-limited (flag ON, `GET /api/v1/app/version` ×12 → 200 ×12, with either middleware). The retro fix (R-MAIN, **MERGED #187 2026-09-24**) swaps in `SlowAPIASGIMiddleware` so those 3 routes 429 with the envelope + `Retry-After` instead of slowapi's bare body; on the pinned slowapi that middleware re-sends `http.response.start` before EVERY body chunk, so every route the default can reach must stay single-chunk (pinned: `/health`, `/`, `/favicon.ico`, + the four docs routes off Railway) — widening the default to router routes or adding an undecorated multi-chunk app-level route is follow-up W1-9d, not a config flip. The two further corrections LANDED: the W1-4 row above is corrected in place (R-AUTH #194); the migration apply order is `037 any time (it depends on nothing unapplied: statement 4 is guarded by to_regprocedure); 036 when its flag is readied — re-run the idempotent 037 after a late 036; 040 any time (run its header census first); 038 = W3-16; 039 reserved for the M13-29 RLS migration` (R-MIG #189: 037 rewritten, 040 added, 036 revokes anon; the old `035 -> 036 -> 037` prerequisite is withdrawn).

**SESSION 67 flags (2026-09-24; every row default OFF, read PER CALL via `os.getenv`, Railway READ via the CLI — `ENABLE_BRIGHTDATA_BUDGET_GATE=true` SET on `web` + `price-warmer` on 2026-09-24 by the orchestrator on Ahmed's delegation, every other new name unset; production had no live deployment from 2026-09-21 (the Railway trial expired) until it was RESTORED on 2026-09-24 and main `dff65210` went live the same day — see the SESSION 67 Active-runtime block; merged #192 W4-2 → #196 R-W04 alongside the retro wave).**
- `ENABLE_SHOPPING_DISCOVERY_URL_SPLIT` (W4-2, `PO-RECORDED-MEASURED-03`, `app/services/price_service.py::shopping_discovery_url_split_enabled`, PR #192) — **TRUE only when `ENABLE_SHOPPING_CURRENCY_TRUTH` is also true (ruling R1)**, both read per call. **Effect ON:** the Serper-shopping rung never puts a SEARCH link in `price["url"]` — a listing `link` (rung 1) or the synthesized `build_retailer_url` fallback (rung 2) rides in the PRIVATE key `_discovery_url` with `url: None`, a real PDP link is untouched (rung 3); the stash mirror `_seed_shortcircuit_candidates` applies the same splitter; the tier-7 `converted_fallback` backfill never re-mints a url for a row carrying the key (`_discovery_url_of` reads the key with NO flag read, ruling R3, so a mid-request rollback cannot re-mint one); `public_price_view` and `GET /api/v1/text/prices/{product}` strip ONLY `_discovery_url` under the exact gate; one `[SHOPPING_DISCOVERY_URL] split url->_discovery_url host= reason=` INFO line for `best`. **Flag OFF:** byte-identical to `6ab9d7ea` (corpus base → head → base2, record by record; the harness never enters the shopping rung — stated). **Consequence:** rows from the 9 non-listing-template retailers (amazon, amazon.ae, amazon.sa, target, newegg, adorama, apple, ebay, aliexpress) lose their 24 h/7 d cache (a fabricated url must never be cached); with `ENABLE_EXACT_PRICE_GATE=false` a discovery-only row becomes cacheable/selectable (pinned as current behaviour). **Activation order for the shopping tier:** W4-1 → W4-2 → W4-3; `ENABLE_REGION_CURRENCY_GUARD` LAST. **THE FLIP IS GATED ON AHMED'S PRODUCT CALL** with the "(converted from USD)" rendering in front of him (W4-1 ruling R5). Follow-ups: `PO-RECORDED-MEASURED-03b` (amazon.ae/amazon.sa collide to amazon.com in `RETAILER_SEARCH_URLS`), 03c (`google.com/shopping/product/<id>` links are not caught by `_is_listing_url`), the pre-existing `_cached` leak on `/text/prices`.
- `ENABLE_CAMERA_FAILURE_ENVELOPE` (R-METER, W2-1b, `app/api/image_routes.py::camera_failure_envelope_enabled`, PR #195; **ON in prod since 2026-10-03 14:23**, flipped with metering and U13) — **Effect ON:** an UNSUCCESSFUL camera comparison (`compare_from_text` returned `success: False` — TIMEOUT / INSUFFICIENT_DATA / LLM_UNAVAILABLE / CONTENT_UNAVAILABLE or the code-less generic branch) is served as the `comparison_failed` envelope exit 6 already ships (the 97b5f15 client's fall-back-to-text branch handles it) carrying the result's own code (`INTERNAL_ERROR` for a code-less one). **Flag OFF:** today's `action: "comparison"` body, except that a code-less result's `error` (the service's `str(e)`, measured carrying an OpenAI key tail) is the constant `comparison unavailable` — UNFLAGGED (M13-26 class). **The no-bill half lives inside the EXISTING `ENABLE_PAID_ROUTE_METERING`:** an unsuccessful result is a NON-delivery — reserved credit refunded, anon credit refunded once (W2-1d wired `refund_anon_comparison_credit` on the six non-delivery exits + this one), no `record_lifetime_comparison`, no history row, `log_search success=False` — pinned in the both-flags-ON deploy state. **FLIP BOTH TOGETHER:** envelope ON + metering OFF still logs success and writes a history attempt (pinned, by design). Unflagged alongside: `compare_from_urls` never delivers a verdict carrying a non-empty `error` (generate_comparison's except-branch used to ship `success: True` with a fabricated `winner_index 0` and the raw exception text) — it returns `LLM_UNAVAILABLE` and `/url/compare` ships **503** with the constant message through the text route's mapping (a coded failure only; the code-less `<2 products` exit keeps its bare-string 400 — W4-9's codeless allowlist would redact it). Recorded for #128, not built: `/url/compare` has NO anonymous gate; the anon credit on `/image/identify` is debited BEFORE the five image-validation 400s.
- `ENABLE_PRICE_PARSE_OFFLOAD` (W0-4, EXISTING flag; R-W04 PR #196 amends its W0 row above IN PLACE) + NEW knob `PRICE_PARSE_MAX_WORKERS` (default 4, clamped 1..16, unparsable / empty / non-finite -> 4, read ONCE when the price-parse pool is first built - a change needs a restart) - the corrected effect, cap, pool, semaphore, ContextVar contract, corpus proof, residual and stated limits are in the amended W0-4 row; nothing else changes for callers. Flag OFF byte-identical (corpus base == same-env `origin/main`, ON `--compare` OFF 0/1656). Activation stays step (2) of the W0 order.

**SESSION 78 flag (S78-TG, 2026-10-14; default OFF, read PER CALL; a new name, so unset in production by construction (Railway not read); plan fix 3 of `docs/investigations/2026-10-14-session-78-state/COMPARE_QUALITY_PLAN_S78.md`, rulings TG1-TG6 + TG-A1..A7 in that folder's `specs/`):**
- `ENABLE_TIER_FILL_RESIDUAL_GUARD` + knob `TAIL_RESERVE_SECONDS` (S78-TG, `app/services/structured_comparison_service.py::StructuredComparisonService._tier_fill_residual_skip`, decision `_tier_fill_residual_short`, work predicate `_tier_fill_has_missing`; the knob is read per call by `_tail_reserve_seconds()`, default 6.5 s = the measured Phase-1-end -> moderation tail, unset / blank / garbage / inf / nan / <= 0 -> 6.5, and it is the ONE knob plan fix 5 reuses). **Effect ON:** inside `_fetch_product_data` the residual `self._compare_deadline - time.monotonic()` is recomputed immediately before EACH fill; `tier2_fill_non_negotiables` is skipped when it is strictly below `_TIER2_WALL_SECONDS` (4.0) + reserve and `tier3_synthesize_non_negotiables` when strictly below `_TIER3_WALL_SECONDS` (3.0) + reserve (independent decisions; the module constants are read at call time), but ONLY when the tier has a field to fill (the same subtype-filtered non-negotiable list and missing / "N/A" / "" rule the tiers apply, and not under the D1 `ENABLE_SPECS_NO_FABRICATION` veto); a tier with nothing to fill, no deadline (None or missing) or an ample residual is called exactly as at base. Each skip logs ONE INFO `[L2.6] tier2|tier3 skipped residual=<x.x>s product=<slot>` and appends `{product, tier}` to the per-run `_tier_fill_skipped` (reset at both entries), which `_stamp_unit_metadata` writes on all three build paths (REST success, the partial builder, the stream `complete`) as the additive `metadata.tier_fill_skipped` = a sorted (product, tier) copy, present only when a skip happened (the stamp does not re-read the flag); REST and stream are both guarded through the shared `_compare_deadline`. **Flag OFF:** byte-identical: the guard returns False before the deadline, the clock or the knob is read, the base call runs with the base kwargs, no key, no line (pinned by `tests/test_s78_tier_fill_residual_guard.py` R6 / R8-off (a raising deadline stand-in), R12 (per-entry resets), R14 (empty marks / non-dict metadata) and by the nine-digest node `tests/test_s75_fanout_identity.py::test_FY25_flag_off_compare_identity_matches_main`); flag ON with nothing to skip is byte-identical too. **Stated limits:** (1) a skipped Tier 3 (gpt-4o synthesis) loses the spec rows it would have filled (capture 2's only two rows came from Tier 3) AND skips the #59 enriched re-cache, so those rows stay missing on later compares until one reaches Tier 3 in time; `metadata.tier_fill_skipped` is the counter; (2) on the STREAM the deadline is recorded at generator entry while the Phase-1 cap is armed at the gather, so the guard over-skips there by the parse + category time (the Phase-2 guard's FS-R4 property); phones drive REST only, and this must be re-ruled before `ENABLE_EXPO_FETCH_SSE` is ever flipped; (3) one reserve per product, while the pair barrier lets the slower product decide when scoring starts (fix 6 is out of scope); (4) the "has work" predicate restates the tiers' gap rule, and drift fails safe (a no-op tier is called as at base); (5) the mark lists gather slots 0/1, not names; a skipped tier records no `source_trace` entry, no cost and about 0.0 ms in `stage_timings_ms`, so only the mark tells it from a no-op tier; (6) persisted in `full_response.metadata` like `phase2_skipped`, no client render site; (7) no coupling with `ENABLE_PHASE2_RESIDUAL_GUARD` (at `PHASE2_MIN_RESIDUAL_SECONDS` 8 a product with 8-10.5 s left runs Phase 2 and then skips both tiers; fix 2's 12 moves that split; canary reading only); (8) with the flag set in a local `.env`, the existing pin `tests/test_s75_fanout_starve.py::test_T18_phase2_residual_guard[off]` goes red (its `_NoRead` deadline stand-in is now read by this guard too); CI runs flag OFF (follow-up: that file's env isolation). **Canary (plan fix 3):** deploy with the flag OFF first (expect no change), then `run_canary.sh tierguard --form pair --timeout 35` on the plan's four never-compared pairs (headphone, moisturiser, vitamin D3 and the warm iPhone control); watch the `[L2.6] tier2|tier3 skipped` lines and `metadata.tier_fill_skipped`, no `[TIER2_FALLBACK] timeout` after a skip, a verdict + pros on pairs that today end as gather / scoring partials, client wall <= 33 s, and the spec-row count per pair on run 1 vs run 2 (the lost re-cache). **Activation precondition:** the owner's explicit yes after that canary, one change per canary window; rollback = unset the variable (per call, immediate).

**SESSION 79 flag (S78-F8, 2026-10-17; default OFF, read PER CALL; a new name, so unset in production by construction (Railway not read); plan fix 8 of `COMPARE_QUALITY_PLAN_S78.md` (session-78 state folder, docs branch), spec-review rulings R1-R13 and RED-gate rulings G1-G8 in `docs/investigations/2026-10-17-session-79-state/specs/`):**
- `ENABLE_ALL_TIMEOUT_CAP_AS_TIMEOUT` (S78-F8, `app/services/structured_comparison_service.py::StructuredComparisonService._cap_all_data_races_timed_out`; the per-run race record `_phase1_race_outcomes` = the eager done-callbacks `_record_phase1_race` on the specs / price race tasks plus the post-loop block, classifier `_phase1_race_outcome`, reset at both entries; counts `_cap_timeout_log_counts`). **Effect ON:** when the REST `compare_from_text` hard cap fires with no usable partial and the products were resolved (the base INSUFFICIENT_DATA branch), the handler ships the base TIMEOUT body (five keys, `TIMEOUT_FRIENDLY_MESSAGE`, route -> HTTP 503) instead of INSUFFICIENT_DATA (400) when every product the early buffer registered holds no usable specs / price and each of its Phase-1 DATA races (specs when it ran, price always) timed out or was cut by the cap (a race that completed, None included, or raised keeps INSUFFICIENT_DATA; reviews / image_url never count), and logs ONE WARNING after the base `no usable partial` WARNING: `[L2.7] hard-cap: every Phase-1 data race timed out or was cut (products=%d, data races recorded=%d, timed out=%d) for query=%r -- reporting TIMEOUT, not INSUFFICIENT_DATA`. **Flag OFF:** byte-identical: one hoisted flag read per product attaches no callback and writes no record, and the predicate returns False before any state is read (pinned by `tests/test_s78_all_timeout_cap_as_timeout.py` F4a / F4b / F4c, F5-off, F8a, F9, F20[off], by the nine-digest node `tests/test_s75_fanout_identity.py::test_FY25_flag_off_compare_identity_matches_main` and by the existing hard-cap pins `tests/test_compare_timeout_graceful.py`, `tests/test_partial_specs_stash_on_price_timeout.py`, `tests/test_compare_from_text_hard_cap.py`, `tests/test_timeout_partial_integration.py`, `tests/test_http_400_cap_cut_mapping.py`); flag ON off the all-timeout branch is byte-identical too (F13 = the nine FY25 digests with the flag ON). **Stated limits:** (1) the two H6 post-gather guards (REST and stream: both products' Phase 1 returned None after the gather completed under the cap) are out of scope and still return INSUFFICIENT_DATA synchronously; with the 30 s cap they are probably the more common all-timeout path, and `ENABLE_TIER_FILL_RESIDUAL_GUARD` (S78-TG) moves more such compares there = follow-up unit S78-F8b; (2) KPI bucket moves: the text route's `search_logs.error_message` moves from the INSUFFICIENT_DATA sentence to `TIMEOUT_FRIENDLY_MESSAGE` in both W4-13 states (`get_error_stats` buckets by exact text), the camera failure row from "INSUFFICIENT_DATA" to "TIMEOUT", and the admin-only `/text/quick` stays HTTP 400 but its `detail` and failure row move to the TIMEOUT copy; (3) HTTP 400 -> 503 on `/text/compare` GET + POST for these rows (the Railway 5xx rate rises by the same count; 503 is excluded from the Sentry 5xx capture, so no Sentry event); (4) the stream is untouched (its Phase-1 cap already yields STREAM_TIMEOUT; the record is written there and never read); (5) a mid-request flip: ON at task creation / OFF at the handler returns the base body (the flag-first predicate), and OFF at creation / ON at the handler fails closed when a data race landed a key, but a data race that RAISED while the flag was off leaves no key and no record (the base done-callbacks swallow the raise), so that one window reads as cut and reports TIMEOUT (measured by the correctness adversary; a flip lands inside at most one in-flight request); (6) a parked or genuine price on a price-race timeout makes the compare a PARTIAL, never this branch; (7) reviews / image_url never count; (8) a data race that never started (behind the unified search or the drug lookup) counts as cut by the cap, and the line cannot tell "never started" from "running"; (9) these rows lose `api_calls` (the TIMEOUT body never carried it; no route reads it); (10) camera: with `ENABLE_CAMERA_FAILURE_ENVELOPE` ON (prod) no client UI change (every non-outage code already renders 'timeout'), with it OFF the row moves from 'generic' to 'timeout'; (11) refund and metering unchanged; (12) with `include_specs=False` the price race alone decides; (13) with `ENABLE_EARLY_SPECS_STASH` OFF the unit is inert (the handler already returns TIMEOUT there); (14) the TIMEOUT copy says "give it another tap" for a pair whose sources are slow on every attempt (the owner's call before the flip; canary reading: the repeat-query rate after a D5 line); (15) never set the flag in a local `.env`: the unedited pin `tests/test_partial_specs_stash_on_price_timeout.py::TestSyncHardCapSalvagesSpecs::test_both_hang_falls_through_to_insufficient_data` encodes the old rule and goes red (CI runs flag OFF). **Canary:** a unit test (the file above) plus log review of the next natural occurrence: grep `[L2.7] hard-cap: every Phase-1 data race timed out or was cut` and, in the same window, count the H6 `INSUFFICIENT_DATA: both products' Phase 1 fetches returned None` lines to size S78-F8b. **Activation precondition:** unit S78-F8b (the same record and predicate at the two H6 post-gather guards, REST scs H6 and stream H6, on this flag) MERGED first; then the owner's explicit yes after the canary; one change per canary window; rollback = unset the variable (per call, immediate). Merge order: #354, #355, #356, then this unit rebased.

**SESSION 79 flag (B3-B, 2026-10-17; default OFF in code, read PER CALL; `ENABLE_APPLE_TOKEN_REVOCATION` ON on `web` since 2026-10-18 06:20 (session-79 ledger, 06:20 line), with the four `APPLE_SIWA_*` variable NAMES set and the values never recorded; spec `.qa-s79/B3B_APPLE_REVOKE_UNIT_SPEC.md` of the unit worktree with rulings B1-B14 and gate rulings GB1-GB12):**
- `ENABLE_APPLE_TOKEN_REVOCATION` (B3-B, `app/services/apple_siwa_service.py::apple_token_revocation_enabled`, read PER CALL; default OFF) + `APPLE_SIWA_TEAM_ID`, `APPLE_SIWA_KEY_ID`, `APPLE_SIWA_PRIVATE_KEY` (the .p8: PEM as is, PEM with literal `\n`, or base64 of the file; whitespace inside the base64 is ignored), `APPLE_SIWA_CLIENT_ID` (`com.qaren.app`; required, no default). Variable NAMES only here; the values live only in Railway. **Effect ON:** an Apple sign-in that carries `authorization_code` (client B3-A, build 1.0.0 (3) and later) exchanges it once at `appleid.apple.com/auth/token` with a fresh 5-minute ES256 client secret (header `alg` + `kid` only) and, when the `sub` of Apple's id_token equals the request's, upserts the Apple refresh token into `public.apple_siwa_tokens` (migration 044, service-role only; `auth_service.store_apple_refresh_token`, INFO `[SIWA] stored`); account deletion reads that token first, runs the unchanged cascade and cache purge, posts it to `appleid.apple.com/auth/revoke` (two attempts at most, the second only after a transport error, the total bound, 429 or 5xx; no sleep), then runs the unchanged auth delete, whose FK cascade removes the row. Every Apple call is bounded in TOTAL by `asyncio.wait_for` 6 s (`_CALL_DEADLINE`) around httpx `timeout=5.0` (ruling B3): the sign-in cost is at most about 6 s, a deletion at most about 12 s. Every Apple failure is logged at WARNING by type or HTTP status and never fails the sign-in or the deletion. **Flag OFF:** byte-identical (no new call, no table read or write, same bodies; pinned by R19 / R20 of `tests/test_s79_b3b_apple_revoke.py` and the U8b order pin). **HARD PRECONDITIONS:** migration 044 applied BEFORE the flip (flag ON without the table fails soft, so revocation silently never happens); the four variables set on `web`. **Activation order (ruling B4; all of it BEFORE build 3 is submitted, since the flag is a no-op for build-2 clients, which send no code):** (1) the owner creates a Sign in with Apple key for the primary App ID `com.qaren.app` and keeps the .p8 out of the repo and out of chat; (2) apply 044 in the SQL editor with its postcheck (`relrowsecurity` true; `has_table_privilege` for service_role INSERT and SELECT true, for anon SELECT false); (3) set the four variables on `web` (base64 on Windows: `[Convert]::ToBase64String([IO.File]::ReadAllBytes('AuthKey_<KEYID>.p8'))` in PowerShell); (4) flip the flag; (5) canary on the TestFlight install of build 3 with a test Apple ID: sign in with Apple -> `[SIWA] exchange status=200` and `[SIWA] stored`, no `store failed`; delete the account -> `[SIWA] revoke attempt=1 status=200`; Settings > Apple ID > Sign in with Apple no longer lists MYEZ; only then submit. **Stated limits:** accounts that signed in with Apple before B3-A + this flag hold no token and cannot be revoked server-side (TN3194 fallback: the user removes the app under Settings > Apple ID > Sign in with Apple); a user who never signs in again after the flip has no token (the deletion-time re-authentication is follow-up unit B3-C; no user-facing text claims revocation until this flag is ON in production); the OpenAPI schema gains the optional `authorization_code` property in both flag states (unflagged; `/docs` is off in production); a rollback to OFF while rows exist skips the revoke and the FK cascade then drops those rows, so those links stay at Apple; development clients with another bundle id (Expo Go) get `[SIWA] exchange status=400`, harmless; Apple's acceptance of the client secret and the revoke semantics cannot be tested offline (the canary is the proof). **Test hazard (gate ruling GB4):** `APPLE_SIWA_PRIVATE_KEY` is in `tests/_env_safety.CREDENTIALS_UNSET`, so every import-time `load_dotenv(override=True)` (`app.main`, the extraction services) re-runs the credential sanitizer and strips it again: a test sets the key only AFTER the first app import (the B3-B test file's autouse fixture imports `app.main` first). **Rollback:** unset the flag (the table can stay; dropping it is `migrations/rollback/044_apple_siwa_tokens.sql`, flag off first).

**SESSION 80 flag (B1, 2026-10-20; default OFF, read PER CALL; a new name, so unset in production by construction (Railway not read); PLAN_S80 unit B1 as amended by RULINGS_R0 R0-1/R0-2/R0-4/R0-5/R0-6/R0-7, RULINGS_BATCH1 B1-R1..B1-R12, the RED gate GATE_B1 GA-1..GA-9 and the fix-round rulings RULINGS_FIX1_B1 F1-1..F1-7 / D1-1..D1-10 (`docs/investigations/2026-10-19-session-80-state/`); spec `.qa-s80/B1_UNIT_SPEC.md` of the unit worktree):**
- `ENABLE_RESULT_STATUS_METADATA` (B1, `app/services/result_gate.py::result_status_metadata_enabled`; the pure module (stdlib + `text_sanitize.dedup_brand_name`, nothing that does I/O) holds `is_placeholder_identity`, `same_identity` (R-A5 with the brand condition of R0-5; a size / variant / distinguishing_features axis read on BOTH sides with different values is never the same identity), `identity_coherence`, `evidence_coverage` under the AUDITABLE and the LITERAL readings, `section_status`, `assess_result`, `telemetry_tags` / `telemetry_extras`, `stamped_product_categories`, `stamp_camera_stage_timings`; identity and rating axes read the RAW `products` alias first and the `overview` mirror only when the alias is absent (B1-R7)). **Effect ON:** every text and camera body built by `build_comparison_response` carries `metadata.result_status` {supported_result, partial_no_winner, needs_identification, unsupported_category, failed}, `identity_confidence` {high, medium, low, unresolved}, `data_coverage` (0-4, the AUDITABLE count: both prices showable and not estimated; both ratings real; a real two-sided spec row that is not model knowledge (a tier-3 synthesis or a `training_data` field, R-A4 / F1-3; the retrieved `smart_fallback` / `tier2_fallback` rows count), NA tokens matched with `strip().lower()` (B1-R2); a non-sentinel, non-low dimension with margin >= 3) and `data_coverage_literal` (the literal count: R0-5 measured 4/27 vs 1/27 corpus rows at zero, so production sizes the gap before B2 flips), `product_categories` (the per-product categories captured BEFORE the pair-category write-back), `section_status` {specs, reviews, pros_cons, price: available | partial | unavailable}, `overview.winner.supported` (bool), and `metadata.winner_index = None` on every non-supported body (the first writer of that key, MYEZ-027: the History list renders such a row with no winner highlight and placeholder tiles; `/home/savings`, `/home/smart-pick` and the three profile readers (`/profile/monthly-stats` savings, `/profile/recent-decisions` names and image tiles; B1-R11 Q7 / F1-2) skip stamped non-supported rows by `result_status`, never by `winner_index`, through the ONE UNFLAGGED reader `home_routes._result_status_supported` (imported by `profile_routes`), so an unstamped row counts exactly as today and a stamped row stays skipped after a rollback; the `/home/savings` skip holds on the legacy Python path only, see the stated limits). One PII-free Sentry info message `comparison_result_status` per DELIVERY exit (text POST / GET, a stream `complete` the client received, camera success, URL success) with the tags `input_type, result_status, identity_confidence, reasons, unknown_product_count, winner_emitted, image_present_a/b, price_showable_a/b, partial_stage, data_coverage, data_coverage_literal` (enums / small ints, grammar `^[a-z0-9_,]+$`; `partial_stage` carries one of the server's stages gather / post_gather / scoring / verdict, `none` when the body is not a partial, and is omitted for an unknown value, F1-6) plus `vision_ms` / `compare_ms` as BUCKET tags (the bucket's lower edge in ms as a digit string: 1000-ms steps for vision, 5000-ms steps for compare; GA-2) with the raw ints as `extras` (a non-finite, bool or str wall is absent, F1-5); the assessment is recomputed from the body with the STAMPED `product_categories` (one reader, `result_gate or carries no str stage (the only writer, the partial builder, always stamps one of the four constants, so that case is unreachable in production).stamped_product_categories`; F1-1: the tags equal the stamp, so `unsupported_category` and `category_conflict` reach Sentry), while `partial`, `partial_stage` (whitelisted) and the `elapsed_ms` fallback are read from `metadata` (D1-2); a non-success body never emits (B1-R6); the call is synchronous inside one try/except (ONE WARNING `[RESULT_STATUS] telemetry skipped: <TypeName>`), never a `fire_and_forget` label, so the W4-13 ledger is untouched. `vision_ms` / `vision_moderation_ms` / `compare_ms` are written as ints into an EXISTING camera `metadata.stage_timings_ms` dict only (R0-6 B1-TIMINGS: production runs `DEBUG_STAGE_TIMINGS=true`, so a presence-only rule would have moved production camera bytes at merge). `sentry_service.compare_traces_sampler` (installed by `init_sentry` beside the kept `traces_sample_rate=0.1`) samples `/api/v1/text/compare`, `/api/v1/text/compare/stream`, `/api/v1/image/identify` and `/api/v1/url/compare` at 1.0 even when the parent trace was not sampled (deliberate: the RN SDK drops 90 % of parents and the point is to see camera traces, so partial traces are expected, D1-4); every other transaction, and every transaction with the flag OFF, keeps the parent decision else the LIVE client `traces_sample_rate` (B1-R1: a harness that forces 1.0 is honoured; 0.1 only when no client carries a rate). Nothing abstains (B2); every new key is ignored by build 3 except that a stamped non-supported History row renders with no winner highlight and placeholder tiles (CR1, pinned by P7; D1-6); the crown (`winner_index`, `overview.winner.product_index`, `scoring_v2.overall_score`, `comparison.winner_index`) is unchanged in both states; a failure inside the stamp leaves the body exactly as with the flag OFF (ONE WARNING `[RESULT_STATUS] assess failed: <TypeName>`). **Flag OFF:** byte-identical on every body and stored row, no message, no timing key, the SDK's own sampling rule (pinned by the nine FY25 digests with the flag in `UNIT_ENV`, `tests/test_s80_b1.py` P1-P8 run with the FULL configuration, the W4-13 ledger, the UR11 bodies, the metering pins); the OFF-state work is the flag read at each site, one unflagged `_result_status_supported` dict lookup per stored row on `/home/savings` (legacy path), `/home/smart-pick` and the three profile readers (an unstamped row counts exactly as today), six `perf_counter` reads on the camera route, the URL wall arithmetic, the per-product category capture in the orchestrator (a two-string list on the partial build ctx, never serialised OFF) and the sampler's flag read plus client-option lookup on every transaction; behaviour unchanged (D1-5). **UNFLAGGED alongside it (R-A1(a), R0-2):** `VALID_EVENT_TYPES` += `scan_start, scan_identity_resolved, scan_comparison_ready, scan_failed, scan_cancelled, image_load_failed` (C7); `VALID_MATTERED_MOST` += `wrong_product, missing_info, unclear_reasons, slow, wrong_winner, unsure` (C6; the third answer state ships as `mattered_most: ["unsure"]`); `FeedbackRequest` gains optional `winner_correct` / `price_correct` / `specs_correct` (`Literal` correct | wrong | unsure, default None; an off-enum value is a 422) written to the live 027 columns only when sent (a 4-field body builds exactly today's record); `mattered_most: []` stays accepted; both lists are append-only (pinned); the widening must be deployed BEFORE any client bundle fires the six event names or the six chip values (the C6 / C7 OTAs after approval). **Stated limits:** `identity_confidence` has no vision signal until B3 threads `assess_result(vision_confidences=...)` (`medium` means an empty brand or no corroborating signal on a product, never "the model was unsure"); `unsupported_category` is reachable only through a per-product category conflict today (every `category_used` is one of the nine), and the per-product stubs recognise generic category words only, so the cross-category rule cannot fire on an explicit pair of brand-only names (GA-1: `product_categories == ['other','other']` on the explicit pair, the LLM per-product category on the parser path and the stream); `failed` is unreachable through any B1 exit (every emitter site is success-only; a direct `assess_result` call only); the URL envelope is never stamped (R0-1, B4) and its telemetry reads a body without prices or `scoring_v2` (status mostly `partial_no_winner`); the stream emits no message when the client left before `complete` (refunded, not delivered); the `/home/savings` skip holds on the legacy Python path only: with `ENABLE_HOME_SAVINGS_AGGREGATE` ON (migration 036, unapplied) the `home_savings_aggregate` SQL keys on `full_response ->> 'winner_index'` and would count stamped rows (a B2 item, D1-1; the smart pick and the profile readers have the Python path only); under the flag the compare transactions rise to 100 % of compare requests AND every delivered compare sends one `info` message that counts as an error event (init sets no `sample_rate`); both Sentry quotas are the owner's reading before the flip (D1-4); never set the flag in a local `.env`: two unedited pins assert the exact `overview.winner` dict and go red with the flag ON (`tests/test_response_builder_winner_card_consistency.py::test_no_mismatch_overview_winner_unchanged`, `tests/test_w4_12_display_contract.py::test_sse_complete_payload_values_unchanged`; measured at the GREEN commit and at the fix HEAD, the only two in the comm set run with the flag ON; CI runs flag OFF); a mid-request flip can yield a stamped body without a message, or the reverse, for at most one in-flight request; stored rows carry the tier-3 marker on 3 of 52 corpus products only, so B2 must honour the STAMPED status, not recompute it on old rows. **Precondition (B1-R12 / CR1):** none to merge; the FIRST flag to flip after approval (R-A2 order: it measures the abstain rate before `ENABLE_RESULT_GATE` abstains); after the flip and before the C1 OTA a stamped non-supported row shows no winner highlight and placeholder tiles in the build-3 History list (HistoryScreen tolerates `winner_index: null`, measured) -- the flag flips only after approval, so no reviewer sees it. **Canary:** the `comparison_result_status` messages grouped by the `result_status` x `input_type` tags over one window (expected from the fixtures: about 15 % `partial_no_winner`, camera-heavy; `needs_identification` on Unknown Product / identical pairs), `data_coverage` vs `data_coverage_literal`, the camera `vision_ms` / `compare_ms` buckets, `/home/savings` and `/profile/monthly-stats` counts unchanged for pre-flip rows, the four compare transactions at 100 % in Performance, zero `[RESULT_STATUS]` WARNING lines in the Railway log. **Rollback:** unset the variable (per call, immediate); rows stamped during the window stay stamped (they stay non-supported) and keep being skipped by Home and Profile. Limit (re-verifier, 2026-10-20): `/profile/recent-decisions` selects its three newest rows BEFORE the skip, so after the flip a user whose three newest comparisons are all stamped non-supported sees the empty state, and a mix shows fewer than three cards, until C1/B2 rework the query.
**SESSION 80 flag (B3 VISION-IDENTITY-V2, 2026-10-20; default OFF, read PER CALL; a new name, so unset in production by construction (Railway not read); spec `.qa-s80/B3_UNIT_SPEC.md` of the unit worktree with review corrections C1-C15, rulings B3-R1..B3-R7, RED-gate rulings GB-1..GB-11 and fix-round rulings F3-1..F3-5 (`specs/RULINGS_FIX1_B3.md`) in `docs/investigations/2026-10-19-session-80-state/`):**
- `ENABLE_VISION_IDENTITY_V2` (B3, `app/services/openai_service.py::vision_identity_v2_enabled`, read per call as the FIRST statement of `identify_products` and once per camera request at the compare site in `app/api/image_routes.py`) + knob `VISION_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` (`vision_timeout_seconds()`, default 20.0 s; unset / blank -> 20.0 silently, garbage / inf / nan / <= 0 -> 20.0 with one WARNING naming the variable; read per call, only under the flag). **Effect ON:** `identify_products` sends a SECOND prompt `VISION_PROMPT_V2` (the exact-label OCR rules kept; no brand or name invented from shape or design: unreadable fields null and confidence low; Arabic-script labels transliterated with the Latin brand kept; the nine canonical categories `VISION_CATEGORY_KEYS` in the A2b order; a 0-based `image_index`) with `response_format` = the strict json_schema `vision_products_v2` (`VISION_RESPONSE_FORMAT_V2`: object `{products:[{image_index, brand, name, category, product_type, size_or_count, visible_price, distinguishing_features, is_product, confidence}]}`, the category branch `anyOf [{type string, enum of the nine}, {type null}]`), `max_tokens` 1000 (`_VISION_V2_MAX_TOKENS`; V1 keeps 500) through the same `token_limit_kwargs` / `sampling_kwargs` / `guarded_llm_create` chokepoint, under `asyncio.wait_for(VISION_TIMEOUT_SECONDS)`; the reply may be the object or a bare array; a second normaliser drops non-dict items and `is_product` false candidates, keeps ONE best candidate per `image_index` (highest confidence, ties keep the earlier) and the best TWO when the request carries exactly one image, and keeps a candidate whose `image_index` does not name one of the request's images (out of range, negative, bool, str, missing) UNPAIRED with `_image_index` None after the paired picks in reply order; confidence is lower-stripped and a missing or unrecognised value -> `low` (V1: a missing key -> `medium`, an unrecognised value kept verbatim), category canonicalised (empty / null -> None), strings stripped (empty -> None; brand / name keep the V1 defaults `Unknown` / `Unknown Product`), features stripped, cut at 80 characters, at most 8. Each product keeps the five public keys (`brand, name, size_or_count, visible_price, confidence`) and carries `_image_index, _category, _product_type, _distinguishing_features, _is_product` internally; the route strips every underscore key from every wire body (`public_vision_products`, unconditional and a no-op on V1 products: `metadata.identified_products`, the `need_second_product` / `comparison_failed` `products` and both failure-envelope sites) while `compare_from_text` receives the un-stripped list; the orchestrator's vision identity dicts (REST and stream twins, `structured_comparison_service._vision_identity_fields`, presence-gated on `_is_product`, no flag read) gain `_vision_confidence`, `_vision_category`, `_vision_distinguishing_features`, `_vision_is_product`, `_vision_image_index` for B2 (never on the wire: `_fetch_product_data` builds a fresh dict). A refused / None-content / choice-less reply raises `LLMUnavailableError` and a vision expiry raises `TimeoutError`; the route serves both as its EXISTING coded 503 `LLM_UNAVAILABLE` (never `TIMEOUT`, whose build-3 tap-to-retry would re-send the photos and re-spend a credit; never a 500), both refunds, no compare; a `finish_reason=length` reply is the V1-shaped parse failure (the existing `action:"error"` exit, refunded). **A signed-in scan under ON runs the text route's full personalisation:** the route forwards `user_id` and, only when `preferences_completed`, `user_preferences` as CONDITIONAL kwargs (`_camera_user_preferences`: one `get_user_preferences` read, sequential, before the compare; a raising read logs `[IMAGE] preferences read failed: <Type>` and the scan compares without preferences); with `user_id` the orchestrator also reads `_fetch_behavior_profile` + `get_user_demographics` (concurrent with the gather) and fires the `_update_behavior_profile` WRITE after a delivered compare; the verdict prompt gains the cohort thin context and `scoring_method` may read `personalized` / `behavioral` / `cohort` on camera rows; no `selected_category` (the route has none; adding one re-records the T23 fixture, B8's job). Log lines (INFO / WARNING, ints, bools, the finish_reason literal and type names only, never a product string): `[VISION_V2] candidates= kept= dropped_not_product= unpaired_index= dropped_non_dict= unknown_brand= unknown_name= images= finish_reason=`, `[VISION_V2] no content: finish_reason= refused=`, `[VISION_V2] finish_reason=length; ...`, `[VISION_V2] vision call timed out after <x.x>s`. **Client-visible changes under ON (measured; the public key set and types unchanged):** the identified SET and therefore `action` can differ (one product per image, unpaired candidates last, `is_product` false dropped, so a scan V1 compared can end in `need_second_product` or `action:"error"`, and the reverse); a refused / None-content reply is the coded 503 `LLM_UNAVAILABLE` where V1 returned a 500; a vision call slower than the knob is a 503 where V1 had no route-level bound (only the SDK client's 120 s per-attempt timeout and its retries); a whitespace-only brand / name reads `Unknown` / `Unknown Product` (V1 kept the whitespace; a null one reads the same default in both), strings are stripped (an empty size / price -> None, V1 kept `""`) and confidence is case-folded (a missing one `low`, V1 `medium`; an unrecognised one `low`, V1 verbatim) in `metadata.identified_products` and the `need_second_product` `products`; a signed-in scan gets the full personalised result (scores, `scoring_method`, the personalization block). **Flag OFF:** byte-identical: the base prompt (sha256 `bb737687...`, pinned through the real function with a fake client), the base message layout and kwargs `{model, messages, max_tokens 500, temperature 0, store False}` (no `response_format`, no `wait_for`, no knob read), the base five-key normaliser (a None content still raises as at base, R-A1), the base six-key identity dicts, the base three-kwarg camera call with no preferences read, and the base bodies (`tests/test_s80_b3_vision_identity_v2.py` P1-P10 + the W2-1 camera body pins in `tests/test_retro_w2_1.py`). **Alongside, test-side (R-A14):** the three `tests/test_camera_vision.py::TestIdentifyProductsMocked` nodes use a `types.SimpleNamespace` usage and run in CI again; `tests/.pre_impl_failures.txt` is 8 rows (per-row prune with pass proof) and its mirror is regenerated; the flag joins the four clear-lists (`test_model_config_enforced` `_clean_env`, the w4_11 recorder `FLAGS`, `test_prompt_fence` `_W411_FLAGS`, the U13 `CLEAN_ENV`; the W4-11 digest fixture and the T23 OpenAPI fixture are untouched). **Fix-round pins (F3-1, 2026-10-20):** the unit file holds 131 nodes; besides P1-P10 / R1-R23 it pins that the preferences read never runs on a non-delivery camera exit and runs once, after `identify_products`, on delivery; that a `finish_reason=length` reply with null content and a refusal carrying string content are both no-content replies (`LLMUnavailableError`, the coded 503); one base64 encode per image in both flag states; the V2 cache telemetry; the timed-out WARNING only on the bound's expiry (a `BadRequestError` keeps its 500, a 429 its coded 503); and `VISION_PROMPT_V2` by sha256 `7d7f2c83...` (`VISION_PROMPT_SHA_ON`) plus its 0-based / numbered-from-0 / no-invention / low-confidence rule substrings, so a deliberate prompt edit re-pins that literal in the same PR. **Stated limits:** (1) whether the live vision model accepts strict structured outputs together with image input is unmeasured offline; a `BadRequestError` on `response_format` takes today's non-transient 500 branch until the flag is unset (no message-text fallback); (2) a 1-based `image_index` reply degrades to V1 pairing (index 1 paired with the second photo, index 2 unpaired), counted by `unpaired_index`; (3) no product-count cap: the prompt bounds the reply at 4 and the route compares `products[0]` and `products[1]`, extra products reach only `metadata.identified_products` (GB-2); (4) `is_product` false candidates are dropped, so B2 sees only their absence; (5) V2 alone still compares a low-confidence pair (V04) and a same-identity pair (V07): the fields are carried, the refusal is B2's (`ENABLE_RESULT_GATE`); V2's no-invention rule can RAISE the `Unknown` / `Unknown Product` share against V1 (the `unknown_brand` / `unknown_name` counters size it), and such a placeholder pair (`Unknown Unknown Product vs ...`) still runs the full paid Serper + LLM pipeline and is delivered and billed until `ENABLE_RESULT_GATE` flips (several R-A2 windows later); (6) the `wait_for` cancels only the awaiting coroutine: OpenAI may complete and bill the request, the SDK's own retries sit inside the one bound, and the breaker records nothing on the cancellation, so a wedged OpenAI shows as camera 503s, not a trip; (7) the cost figure stays the mini list price whatever `OPENAI_MODEL_VISION` resolves to (`metadata.openai` is the priced figure); (8) a signed-in scan adds the preferences read before the compare (inline on the loop while `ENABLE_SYNC_DB_OFFLOAD` is OFF) plus the behaviour-profile / demographics reads and the profile write above; (9) the stream twin is pinned by source text only (the camera route is REST-only); (10) `metadata.identified_products` is a copy, no longer the same list object as the `vision_products` argument (equal by value); (11) Arabic transliteration quality and whether the model keeps a Latin brand as printed are unmeasured offline; (12) `OPENAI_MODEL_VISION` is UNKNOWN on `web` (R-A15): the call shape is the same for every id, structured outputs on a GPT-5 id are untested here; (13) the exit-2 copy for a `finish_reason=length` reply blames the photo for a token cap (the client copy is C1's); (14) with `ENABLE_VISION_IDENTITY_V2` exported in a developer or CI environment, the three mocked `test_camera_vision. the extra attempts target neutralized.supabase.invalid, the sentinel class of scripts/netguard_ratchet.py (a new sentinel-only node prints a WARNING and exits 0), not egress.env`; CI runs flag OFF; (15) on a two-photo request where the model reports both products at `image_index` 0, V2 keeps ONE product (the best of index 0) and the scan ends in `need_second_product` where V1 compared both; a close-up of one of them as photo 2 can therefore turn a V1 pair into a same-identity pair under ON (B3-R6(2) stands; B2 may revisit). **HARD PRECONDITION before the flip:** from a Railway shell on `web` (or locally with the production `OPENAI_API_KEY` name present), run ONE off-route smoke: `ENABLE_VISION_IDENTITY_V2=true python -c "..."` calling `app.services.openai_service.identify_products` with two real product photos (the env var exported for that process only; the web service's variables untouched). Expected: no `BadRequestError` on `response_format`, a `[VISION_V2] candidates=` INFO line, two products with `_image_index` 0 and 1 (the internal key `identify_products` returns), and `_category` from the nine keys or null. A 400 on the schema means the strict-subset literal is rejected by the model: do not flip; open an issue. The owner never flips to test. **Activation (R-A2):** after `ENABLE_RESULT_STATUS_METADATA` and before `ENABLE_IMAGE_PIPELINE_V2`, one canary window, the owner's explicit yes: the off-route smoke above first (run on `web`, it resolves the same `OPENAI_MODEL_VISION` as the service), flip, then two never-compared photo pairs (a labelled pair and a label-less pair) watching the `[VISION_V2]` counts (`unpaired_index`, `unknown_brand`, `unknown_name`), the `need_second_product` count under ON against the V1 baseline (limit 15), billed camera compares whose `[VISION_V2]` line shows `unknown_name` > 0 (limit 5), the 503 `LLM_UNAVAILABLE` rate and the `action:"error"` rate on `/image/identify`, p50 vision latency against the 20 s knob (on a V2 expiry the route's `[IMAGE] Vision call failed: ` line has an empty tail, so the `[VISION_V2] vision call timed out` WARNING is the only discriminator), the camera `scoring_method` mix, the `behavior_profile_update` WARNING count and the Railway log for `BadRequestError`. **Rollback:** unset the variable (per call, immediate).

**SESSION 80 flag (B4 URL-MODE-V2, 2026-10-20; default OFF, read PER CALL; a new name, so unset in production by construction (Railway not read); PLAN_S80.md section 3 B4 + R-A7 as amended by RULINGS_R0.md R0-1 / R0-6 / R0-7, rulings B4-R1..R11 of RULINGS_BATCH1.md, RED-gate rulings GC-1..GC-8 of `specs/GATE_B4.md` fix-round rulings F4-1..F4-6 of `specs/RULINGS_FIX1_B4.md`, F2-1..F2-6 of `specs/RULINGS_FIX2_B4.md`, F3-1..F3-7 of `specs/RULINGS_FIX3_B4.md` and the fix-round-4 rulings F4-1..F4-6 of `specs/RULINGS_FIX4_B4.md`; spec `.qa-s80/B4_UNIT_SPEC.md` of worktree sc-s80-b4, copied to `docs/investigations/2026-10-19-session-80-state/specs/`):**
- `ENABLE_URL_MODE_V2` (B4, `app/services/url_extraction_service.py::url_mode_v2_enabled` + the local one-liner `app/middleware/error_handler.py::_url_mode_v2_keys_enabled` reading the same name; the route reads it ONCE per request and threads a bool down as the conditional kwarg `url_mode_v2=True`; module constant `url_routes._URL_COMPARE_DEADLINE_SECONDS` 25.0, not an env knob). **Effect ON:** POST/GET `/api/v1/url/compare` refuse for $0 BEFORE the credit reserve (after the unchanged SSRF 400) a link longer than 2,048 characters (the client's validateUrl bound, `url_routes._URL_V2_MAX_LINK_CHARS`; checked FIRST, before any query parse, reason `url_too_long`, F4-1), a link whose PATH is a storefront root (`/`, `/en`, `/en-bh/`, `/index.html`; not when its query names a product route: an OpenCart `route=product/product` (exactly that value, case-insensitive, F3-2: a listing route such as `product/category`, `product/search` or `product/compare` stays a homepage unless the link also carries a non-empty `product_id` / `id_product` / `product` / `products_id`, so `/index.php?route=product/category&product_id=5` and even a cart route such as `/?route=checkout/cart&product_id=5` are fetched, and a cart page is then refused only after the paid LLM rung) or `product_id`, a PrestaShop `id_product`, a WooCommerce `product` or an osCommerce / Zen Cart `products_id` key (F4-3; Zen Cart's default PDP url is `index.php?main_page=product_info&products_id=N`), each with a NON-EMPTY value (an empty value is no marker, F3-4), keys case-insensitive, an explicit allow-list (F2-1), so a tracking-only query such as `?gclid=` / `?utm_source=` stays a homepage), a search route, a cart/checkout route, an account route or a category/collection listing without a product segment, and a pair equal after normalisation (scheme/host casefold, one leading `www.`, the trailing slash and the fragment dropped, and every query key dropped except the product-route keys with their values, `route=product/product`, `product_id`, `id_product`, `product` and the osCommerce `products_id`, keys case-insensitive, values percent-decoded and stripped, F3-1: two products of ONE query-routed store are not the same url, a tracking-only difference still is; each link's query is parsed ONCE per request for both the class and the key, `url_pre_gate_view`, F4-1): HTTP 400 `code: NOT_PRODUCT_PAGE` with the additive `field` (url1|url2|both) and `reason` (url_too_long|homepage|search|cart|account|category|same_url), no reserve, no search_logs row (the SSRF-400 class). After the fetch (a second fetch function `_fetch_page_v2` that keeps the last status and the final url; `fetch_page` byte-unchanged): a transport error, an SSRF-blocked initial url or hop, the redirect cap (at most five requests, four followed hops, as `fetch_page`; pinned F2-4d), a non-2xx (fragrancex 403 = V02) or a challenge page judged on the start of its FIRST `<title>` (read by a linear `str.find` scan of the first 64 KiB only (pinned F3-5), an unclosed title up to 1,024 characters; F2-2 replaced a quadratic regex that froze the event loop about 2.5 s per crafted link) -> 400 `STORE_UNREADABLE` (reason fetch_error|blocked|redirect_cap|http_403|http_4xx|http_5xx|http_3xx|challenge; a 401 is `http_4xx`, pinned F3-5); a FINAL url in any pre-gate class (a redirect to the storefront root, a search, cart/checkout, account or category route) -> 400 `NOT_PRODUCT_PAGE` with that class as the reason (GC-2, every class pinned F2-4e; a final url whose query names a product route is not a homepage, F2-1); a page with no schema.org Product node (found through the JSON-LD shape ladder: `@graph`, `@type` lists, `ProductGroup.hasVariant`; the first node in document order gives the name, price and image, pinned F2-4f) and no usable title (the meta title is the first USABLE of og:title and the `<title>` tag, F4-4, so an og:title equal to the site name or a placeholder no longer shadows a usable `<title>`; unusable = empty, a placeholder, or equal to an Organization / WebSite name or og:site_name; a WebPage node's name is not a site name, pinned F2-4b) -> 400 `UNRESOLVED_PRODUCT` `no_product_node` with NO LLM call (V09); the extraction LLM's `is_product_page: false` refuses (`not_product_page`) only on a page with no Product node, and `null_name` only when the title is unusable (ruling B4-R1); a delivered `products[i].name` is never a placeholder or the site's own name (F2-5): a Product node whose name is missing or unusable takes that first usable meta title (decided on the value, not on key presence, so a nameless node with a price delivers at $0 with no LLM call), an unusable merged LLM name gives way to the usable title, the title is the first USABLE of the page title and the LLM's `full_title` (a placeholder-named or site-named node no longer shadows a usable `full_title`, F3-3), and with no usable title the link is `UNRESOLVED_PRODUCT` `null_name`; a 25 s `asyncio.wait_for` around `compare_from_urls` -> the structured 503 `TIMEOUT`. Two links failing differently: STORE_UNREADABLE > NOT_PRODUCT_PAGE > UNRESOLVED_PRODUCT with `field: both` and the reason of the first link carrying the chosen code (pinned F2-4f); an exception inside `extract_from_url` keeps the base codeless 400. Every one is a non-delivery (R-A3) through the existing coded-failure exit of `_compare_urls_metered`: refund once (`usage_refund.url.compare.failure`), no `record_lifetime_comparison`, no history row, the W4-13 failure row with the code's constant message (three copy constants in the service). The error handler passes `field` / `reason` (str values only) through ONLY for the three B4 codes (ruling B4-R5). The extractor normalises `image_url` to the first absolute https url string (JSON-LD `image` through `price_service._jsonld_image_urls`, `urljoin` against the FINAL url, the og:image key-presence bug fixed; a list never ships: a deliberate retype of the raw JSON-LD list on fragrancex-class pages, PLAN section 0 item 4), fills `recommendation` from `strip_score_internals(winner_reason)` with the `_QUALITATIVE_WINNER_REASON` fallback and `key_differences` from the `specs_comparison` advantages (frozen keys, no new top-level key, still no `metadata`: Link compares stay unpersisted, R0-1), and asks the extraction LLM for `is_product_page` through `URL_EXTRACTION_PROMPT_V2` (derived from the untouched V1 by two count-checked swaps, same render shape, same kwargs {gpt-4o-mini, 800, 0.1, store False}). Logs (constants, ints, exception type names and hosts only): INFO `[URL-V2] refused code=<c> field=<f> reason=<r>`; INFO `[URL-V2] outcome=<o> reason=<r> status=<n>` once per fetched link, outcome in ok|fetch_error|blocked|redirect_cap|http_error|challenge|not_product_page|unresolved and reason the wire reason or `delivered`; `[URL-V2] fetch failed host=<h> reason=<r> type=<ExceptionType or None>` once per unreadable fetch: at ERROR for a transport error (`reason=fetch_error`, logged inside `_fetch_page_v2`'s except arm with the exception TYPE; RULINGS_FIX1_B4 F4-1 amending GC-1), at WARNING for a blocked initial url or a blocked hop, the redirect cap, a non-2xx or a challenge page (`type=None`); never the base `Failed to fetch URL host` ERROR template, which stays the one ERROR call with that prefix (the u8d R8 pin); WARNING `[URL-V2] deadline 25.0s hit`; the v2 fetch's `[SSRF] Blocked initial URL: host=` / `[SSRF] Blocked redirect to host=` / `[SSRF] Too many redirects for host=` WARNING lines print the host only (pinned F2-4a: no record of any logger carries a pasted userinfo, path or query token on any unreadable outcome; the transport ERROR record carries no exception info, exception text or stack info, pinned F3-5 on that record, and no record of the run carries exception info). Sentry consequence (F2-6 a, the cause per F3-6; read from code, no Sentry canary): an ERROR log record becomes a Sentry event through the default `event_level=logging.ERROR` of the `LoggingIntegration` that `sentry_service` installs (its `level=logging.ERROR` argument on the `LoggingIntegration(...)` call in `sentry_service.py` (cited by name: the line moved from :651 to :736 when B1 #364 landed), sets only the breadcrumb level), so with the flag ON a link transport error is a Sentry event under the `[URL-V2] fetch failed` template (it replaces the base `Failed to fetch URL host` event of the OFF path, so the count is unchanged for transport errors), while a non-2xx link (the V02 fragrancex class), an ERROR event at base through `raise_for_status`, becomes a WARNING and no Sentry event (the `[URL-V2] outcome=http_error` INFO line and the search_logs failure row are its counters). **Flag OFF:** byte-identical (the route makes the base positional call, no pre-gate, no deadline, `fetch_page`, the base prompt, the raw `image_url`, `recommendation ""` / `key_differences []`, the four-key error envelope; pinned under the full configuration (ruling B1) by `tests/test_s80_url_mode_v2.py` P5, P6, P15, P17, P20, P23, P25, P36, the R32 OFF half, the R38 OFF half and R39, and by `tests/test_retro_w2_1.py`, the `url.*` rows of `tests/fixtures/w4_13_flag_off_ledger.json`, the W4-11 `url_extract_kwargs` and the T23 OpenAPI fixture); the only OFF deltas are one env read per request in the route and one per error response, the import-time derived `URL_EXTRACTION_PROMPT_V2`, and defaulted kwargs on `extract_from_url`, `compare_from_urls`, `extract_with_ai` and `normalize_product_data` (non-wire). **UNFLAGGED (R-A1 b, log-only):** the INFO lines `URL comparison request`, `URL extraction request` and `Detected retailer` print the host only (the `Detected retailer` label is a SUPPORTED_RETAILERS name or `unknown`, never the raw netloc, which can carry a pasted `user:pass@`), and `extract_with_ai`'s ERROR line is `AI extraction error: <ExceptionType>`, the exception TYPE only, never its text (F4-5, engineering e-m6; reachable flag OFF and ON; the returned `{"error": str(e)}` is unchanged). **Build 3:** every new 400 code renders the generic "try with brand or model" copy (`errorCopy.ts` default arm) exactly as the codeless 400 does today, and the 503 TIMEOUT the timeout copy; `field` / `reason` are read by C3 after approval. **HARD PRECONDITIONS:** none to flip (no migration, no variable); activation only after App Store approval in the R-A2 order (after `ENABLE_CLAIMS_PROVENANCE`, before `ENABLE_RESULT_GATE`), the owner's explicit yes, one change per canary window. **Stated limits:** (1) a single-page store whose product lives at the root, or a locale root that 302s to a PDP, is refused as a homepage (a root or /index.* path whose query names a product route is exempt, F2-1: an OpenCart `route=product/product` only, so an OpenCart listing route on a root or /index.* path, `product/category`, `product/search`, `product/compare`, `product/special` or `product/manufacturer`, is refused at $0 as a homepage unless the link also carries a non-empty `product_id` / `id_product` / `product` / `products_id` (then it is exempt and fetched, a cart route such as `/?route=checkout/cart&product_id=5` too, and a cart page is refused only after the paid LLM rung), F3-2, and an allow-listed key with an empty value is no marker, F3-4; the osCommerce / Zen Cart `products_id` is a product-route marker since F4-3), and a WooCommerce product reachable only as `/?p=N` (plain permalinks) or a single ASP.NET-style `?pid=N` link on a root path is refused as a homepage: a bare `p` (a WordPress post id) and `pid` are generic and deliberately not in the allow-list (F2-6 d, F3-1); (2) fragrancex stays unreadable (403 to the plain httpx fetch; no Chrome impersonation, R-A7): a faster, free refusal, still the generic copy until C3; (3) the pre-gate reuses price_service's storefront-root / search regexes plus three narrow ones, so these pass it (pinned as current behaviour, ruling B4-R7): `/ar/search` (the search regex is not locale-prefixed), amazon `/s` (a query-based refusal is out: R-A7 is path-only), noon `/uae-en/search/` and 3-letter locale roots (`/uae-en/`, `/eng/`), `/en/cart.html`; Amazon is then refused post-fetch at $0 (no `productTitle` -> `no_product_node`), noon only after the paid LLM rung; (4) KPI: `search_logs.error_message` gains the three constant messages and the TIMEOUT copy, storefront pairs that counted as successes (V09) become failures, and pre-gate refusals write no row (the SSRF-400 class), so the admin error stats under-count link refusals until B12 (compare the Railway 400 count); (5) the 503 TIMEOUT rows raise the 5xx rate; the deadline cancels `compare_from_urls` mid-flight (a parse job already on the price-parse pool completes orphaned; a cancelled `guarded_llm_create` never trips the OpenAI breaker); (6) the LLM is still called once per link with a usable title and no price (cost unchanged); a page WITH a Product node is never refused on the LLM's `is_product_page: false`; the shape ladder never reaches ItemList members, so an ItemList page has no node and is judged by the title / LLM rungs; (7) https only, no raster check, first image only; the amazon / noon extractors keep their base bodies (their image, and an LLM-merged one, is coerced in the normaliser: pinned by `test_normaliser_coerces_a_raw_image_list_flag_on`) and never report a Product node, so a noon page the LLM calls `false` is refused, and a 200 Amazon interstitial without `productTitle` is `UNRESOLVED` `no_product_node` at $0 (the reason name is approximate there); (8) `recommendation` / `key_differences` come from the verdict prose, not from scoring (B12); (9) two readers of one flag name (service + middleware); (10) the `[SSRF]` WARNING lines of the flag-OFF `fetch_page` still print the full url (follow-up); (11) same_url keeps path case and percent-encoding and ignores default-port equivalence (`:443` vs none differ); it keeps only the product-route query keys (F3-1), so two products of ONE query-routed store (OpenCart `index.php?route=product/product&product_id=` A vs B and the root `/?route=` form, PrestaShop `id_product`, WooCommerce `?product=`, osCommerce `products_id`; measured refused `same_url` at `83daa8cb` by the round-2 re-verifier, now delivered and pinned F3-1) are not the same url, while EVERY same-store pair keyed outside the five allow-listed keys (`route`, `product_id`, `id_product`, `product`, `products_id`) normalises equal and is refused `same_url` at $0 with the store-page copy: two size / variant links of ONE product (`?variant=`, `?attribute_pa_size=`), and two products of one store addressed as ASP.NET `ProductDetails.aspx?pid=` (`pid` is generic: a WordPress post or page id), `product.php?id=`, `ProductDetails.aspx?ProductID=` or `item?sku=` (the last three measured through the real route by the round-3 correctness adversary, c-m2; F2-6 c, F3-1; follow-ups: a tracking-key deny-list (utm_*, gclid, fbclid, ...) in place of the product-key allow-list, and a same_url-specific message); (12) a mid-request flip: the route reads once and threads a bool, the error handler reads at response time (a request that started ON and ends OFF ships the two keys dropped: the base envelope); (13) refunds use the legacy previous-window fallback (`refund_comparison_credit(user_id)` without `consumed_keys`, shared with the text path; ruling B4-R6, B2 threads them); (14) `is_usable_title` strips ASCII punctuation only (an Arabic storefront title is compared casefolded as a whole string), and a WebPage node's name never counts as a site name; (15) `classify_url_path` returns None for a falsy or non-str link (GC-4; unreachable from the route); (16) never set the flag in a local `.env` (CI runs flag OFF): the measured local-.env class, the five `url_compare_*` nodes of `tests/test_paid_route_metering.py` (its `_stub_url_leaves._extract(url)` stub has the base signature), is closed by the test-only clean lists E5 (`tests/test_retro_w2_1.py`) and E6 (`tests/test_paid_route_metering.py`); measured green with the flag exported on the fix-round-4 bytes: `tests/test_s80_url_mode_v2.py` (452 nodes), `tests/test_paid_route_metering.py` (21), the 40 module / symbol readers of the F4-2 comm set (2,320 passed; only the two known cold-tiktoken ERRORs of `tests/test_prompt_truth.py`) and the 20-file pin set (1037 nodes: `test_retro_w2_1`, `test_url_compare_category`, `test_prompt_fence`, `test_model_config_enforced`, `test_s71_u13_compare_auth_required`, `test_w4_13_flag_off_ledger`, `test_w4_13_measurement_truth`, `test_error_middleware`, `test_ssrf_redirect_hardening`, `test_retro_w0_4_efg`, `test_paid_route_metering`, `test_u13e_url_detect_guard`, `test_comparison_id_echo`, `test_u3c_store_false_pin`, `test_sentry_channels_u8d_unit`, `test_429_contract`, `test_retro_w1_9_429`, `test_text_routes_error_mapping`, `test_http_400_cap_cut_mapping`, `test_s70_url_client_retries`); the rest of the comm set was measured with the flag unset (F2-6 a); (17) an inner builtin `TimeoutError` escaping `compare_from_urls` before the 25 s deadline is also reported as the 503 `TIMEOUT` with the deadline WARNING (`asyncio.TimeoutError` is the builtin; none is known, the inner `wait_for` sites catch theirs; no switch to `asyncio.timeout`, RULINGS_FIX1_B4 F4-5); (18) `_fetch_page_v2`'s httpx timeout (30.0 s) exceeds the 25 s compare deadline, so a stalled or tarpitting store always surfaces as the 503 `TIMEOUT` (the retry copy; a retry repeats the 25 s and the reserve / refund), never as `STORE_UNREADABLE` `fetch_error` (read from code; no code change, since lowering it risks slow-but-alive GCC stores: follow-up; F2-6 b); (19) diff notes (F2-6 f): the two 8+-digit runs in the added lines are the `BASE_FETCH_PAGE_SHA` hex constant and the noon path id of a classify row, neither personal; an extra stdlib `import string` and the spec helper `_v2_product_nodes` inlined into `extract_generic_data_v2` are accepted spec-shape deviations; (20) the 2,048-character bound sits INSIDE the flagged pre-gate (F4-1): the SSRF validation and the host-only `URL comparison request` INFO line still read an over-long link first (one linear `urlsplit` each, in both flag states), the request model carries no `max_length` (the OpenAPI schema is unchanged), and the post-fetch final-url check parses the query of one final url, which httpx bounds at 65,536 characters; measured through the test client, two 5 MB links are refused in about 0.09 s per request ON (the pre-gate itself under 50 ms, no query parse; 3.55 s at `0aac07f1`) against 0.06-0.07 s for the base 400 OFF. **Canary (after approval):** Link compares from a TestFlight install: the fragrancex homepage pair -> 400 NOT_PRODUCT_PAGE in under 1 s with 0 credits; a real PDP pair -> 200 with a non-empty `recommendation` and a str `image_url`; a Rasasi PDP pair; an OpenCart query-routed Bahrain PDP pair from the registry (`index.php?route=product/product&product_id=` links) from two DIFFERENT stores AND one pair of two products of ONE OpenCart store -> 200 both (F3-1); then the `[URL-V2]` lines, the url failure mix in `search_logs` and the `/url/compare` 400 / 503 counts. **Rollback:** unset the variable (per call, immediate).

**SESSION 81 flag (D03 DENY-AND-GUARDS, 2026-10-22; default OFF, read PER CALL; a new name, so unset in production by construction (Railway not read); PLAN_S81 unit D03 (M1, lane B) and Fable ruling F1; spec `.qa-s80/D03_UNIT_SPEC.md` of the unit worktree with its adversarial review B1-B5 / RC1-RC12 / C1-C18, rulings `specs/RULINGS_D03.md` (R-D03-1..4, B1-B5, C1-C12), the RED gate `specs/GATE_D03.md` (GC-1..GC-7) and the fix-round rulings `specs/RULINGS_FIX1_D03.md` (F1-1..F1-8) in `docs/investigations/2026-10-22-session-81-state/`):**
- `ENABLE_DATA_GUARDS_V2` (D03, `app/services/deny_hosts.py::data_guards_v2_enabled`; the stdlib-only leaf holds the ONE banned-host list `DENY_HOSTS` = Fragrantica (fragrantica.com and its 20 language editions measured in the held corpora), Parfumo (parfumo.com, parfumo.de), Basenotes (basenotes.com, basenotes.net) and the Fragrantica image CDN fimgs.net, 26 bare registrable domains, no scheme, so the T7b legal scan sees nothing; `is_denied_host` / `is_denied_url` / `is_denied_source` deny a listed domain or any subdomain (www, port, trailing dot and case normalised) AND any host whose registrable LABEL is fragrantica / parfumo / basenotes / fimgs with any public suffix (ruling C10, GC-1: `fragrantica.cn`, `parfumo.it`, `parfumo.co`, `fimgs.network` are denied; `notfragrantica.com`, `fragrantica.com.evil.test` are not; the suffix arm is kept as defence in depth, GC-2); a source NAME is denied when it carries a banned label as a whole token (`Fragrantica`, `Parfumo.de`); every function is pure, flag-free and never raises; `is_denied_url` decides on the parsed HOSTNAME (userinfo and port removed, never the netloc) and denies an authority that carries a backslash whenever a banned label appears in that authority (a WHATWG parser, i.e. a browser or an image loader, reads such an authority differently; fix-round ruling F1-4); the descriptor's two-host tuple moved here as `LEGACY_DESCRIPTOR_DENY_HOSTS` (the same object is `search_descriptor_service.DENY_HOSTS`); the flag-FREE KB-writer guard `kb_source_allowed` / `assert_kb_source_allowed` / `DeniedSourceError` (message the constant `denied source`) has no caller yet; `deny_hosts.py` holds the two deny literal tuples, `DENY_HOSTS` and the re-exported `LEGACY_DESCRIPTOR_DENY_HOSTS`, and no other deny literal list exists in `app/` (pinned by R1i, which scans every module but `deny_hosts.py`); the flag read precedes every behavioural branch at each consumer (a pure base precondition, such as an empty-input check, may run first; the order of the pure deny check and the flag read inside one condition is equivalent by ruling, MIN-E5)). **Effect ON:** (1) `source_router` skips a denied row AT USE in all twelve registry consumers through `_routable_registry()` (selectors, `build_site_discovery_query`, `score_source`, `source_usage`, `registry_tier`, `match_registry_apex`, `is_render_only_domain`, `_registry_row_for_host` and so the descriptor lookup answer exactly as if the row were absent;
