Log lines: `[ROUTER] consent update required site=%s consent_state=%s`, `[ROUTER] consent update required at compare entry`, `[ROUTER] ANTHROPIC_API_KEY unset; anthropic roles unavailable (fail closed)` (once per process), `[ROUTER] anthropic dispatch failed site=%s class=%s status=%s transient=%s`, `[ROUTER] anthropic refusal site=%s`, `[ROUTER] escalation site=%s trigger=%s`, `[ROUTER] anthropic client construction failed site=%s class=%s`, `[ROUTER] moderation step failed class=%s -- failing closed`, `[CIRCUIT] anthropic breaker read failed (<Type>) -- failing open` (constants, ints, type names only). **Flag OFF (every role flag unset / falsy):** byte-identical on every response body and stored row: each site makes the exact base call (one getenv), the moderation call is the base `moderations.create`, no consent read, no llm ledger, no `circuit:anthropic` read, no `[ROUTER]` line, the base cache key and `/health` body (pinned by `tests/test_s81_s06_anthropic_router.py` P01-P13 and `tests/test_s81_s06_router_gate_rows.py` under the FULL configuration, the FY25 nine digests with the seven names in `UNIT_ENV`, the W4-13 flag-OFF ledger); `ENABLE_OPENAI_PRE_V3` or `ENABLE_SONNET_ESCALATION` ON alone is byte-identical too. **Unflagged alongside:** `anthropic==1.12.1` in `requirements.in` (the orchestrator recompiles both locks and runs pip-audit); the `anthropic` row of `app/legal/processors.json` appended at the END (public_name false, category 'third-party AI service', gated by `ENABLE_ANTHROPIC_TEXT`, the EIGHTH fill-in flag, recorded "off" in `tests/fixtures/legal_fill_in_u8.json`); the explicit Sentry integration (`BASE_INTEGRATIONS` lists it unconditionally, so before the PR the orchestrator recompiles both locks with `anthropic==1.12.1`, runs `pip-audit --strict` and installs the pin into the shared `.venv-qaren`; with the SDK installed the explicit `AnthropicIntegration(include_prompts=False)` is active in production without a flag, a no-op until a Claude call is made, F1-16). **Deliberate pin changes:** `tests/test_model_config_enforced.py` (the census accepts `llm_router.route`, floor 16; the clear-list; the census floors COUNT sites: the routing itself is enforced by R04 and `tests/test_s81_s06_router_gate_rows.py::test_g13_every_llm_site_dispatches_through_the_router`, no direct `guarded_llm_create(` call in `app/` outside `llm_router.py` and `api_budget_service.py`, F1-14), `tests/test_u3c_store_false_pin.py` (03: the routed census, floors 16 sites / 33 spreads; 02: the Anthropic Messages surface allowed only inside `guarded_anthropic_create`, plus two arms outside `llm_router.py`: an assignment binding a `messages` / `messages.batches` attribute to a name and the `/v1/messages` REST path string, F1-13), `tests/test_model_config.py` (the `claude-` literal pattern, `llm_pricing.py` allowed), `tests/test_legal_docs_u8.py` (SEVEN_FLAGS eight, `AsyncAnthropic` / `Anthropic` client constructors, the positive control), `tests/fixtures/legal_s78.json` (`manifest_base.anthropic`, R22), `scripts/fill_in_legal.py` (SEVEN_FLAGS eight), `tests/test_sentry_channels_u8d.py` (`AnthropicIntegration` in BASE_INTEGRATIONS, `anthropic_include_prompts` False), `tests/test_s81_s05_request_context.py` (P1: `llm_router` is the first consumer, `from app.utils import request_context`, attributes {consent_version, header_state}), the seven names in the FY25 / w4_11 recorder / prompt-fence / U13 / retro-W2-1 clear-lists; the U3c 01 / 01b / 01c and K1 pins are untouched (`guarded_llm_create` unchanged). **Flip preconditions (ALL):** S81-12 LEGAL-V3 merged and live with the landing (it flips `public_name` and names Anthropic; the known rebase on `processors.json`, `legal_s78.json` `manifest_base` and `PUBLIC_NAME_IDS`); the consent v3 OTA (S81-08) propagated; `ENABLE_REQUEST_CONTEXT_HEADERS` ON first (else every request reads consent None); `ENABLE_LLM_LEDGER_V2` ON (cost truth); `ENABLE_LLM_SPEND_BREAKER` ON before `ENABLE_ANTHROPIC_VERDICT` (PLAN_S81 flip order; GATE_S81_06 GC-11); the S81-07 go per role (latency, refusal rate on fragrance names, the no-format JSON validity and the closed-schema + image-block acceptance, the per-site caps, the Sonnet `between_tools` shape, the three timeout values, the 100-case moderation parity set before `ENABLE_ANTHROPIC_MODERATION`); `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` on `web` by name. **Flip order:** text -> verdict -> critic -> moderation (after parity) -> vision LAST, with the camera permission-string risk accepted or 1.0.1 live (build 3's Info.plist purpose strings say photos go to OpenAI, `app.json:220,228`; the consent sheet v3 and the policy are the disclosures; an OTA cannot change them). **Stated limits:** (1) the consent version is the client's claim; (2) Haiku has no server fallback beyond one Sonnet escalation per compare; the LLM_UNAVAILABLE non-delivery holds at the entry gates and at the verdict site (refund via the existing `refund_comparison_credit(user_id)` call shape; the consumed_keys refund is S81-09's); a Claude refusal or dispatch failure at the PARSE step becomes the codeless parse-failure 400 (refunded; the copy says the query was not understood: S81-11 copy follow-up); at specs / price / reviews / critique it degrades as at base (F1-9); (3) OpenAI moderation runs while the moderation flag is OFF, so OpenAI cannot be fully OFF before parity, and the OpenAI moderations endpoint is not in the preflight AND (not breaker-gated, fails open); (4) the OpenAI free allowance ends with the switch; (5) no web search, streaming, Batch or prompt caching here; (6) the structured-output acceptance of the closed schemas (incl. the V2 `anyOf`) and of the format + image-block combination, and the no-format JSON validity, are unmeasured offline (S81-07); (7) measured on the pinned 1.12.1 SDK: passing `api_key` explicitly stops the SDK's credential discovery and `base_url` is explicit, but the SDK still reads `ANTHROPIC_CUSTOM_HEADERS` (and `ANTHROPIC_WEBHOOK_SIGNING_KEY`) from the environment: never set any `ANTHROPIC_*` name other than the key and the knobs above on `web` or `price-warmer`; (8) the breaker's Redis calls run on the default `qaren-worker` executor through `asyncio.to_thread` (the read-only preflight reads on the loop, once per memo window, as the OpenAI one does); a cross-process trip is seen up to `LLM_BREAKER_CACHE_TTL` late; (9) a mid-request role-flag flip is costed by the scope bound at entry (a flag turned ON mid-request records its Claude calls nowhere); (10) off-clock scripts and the warmer have no request context: with a role flag ON they route by `ENABLE_OPENAI_PRE_V3` (consent None) or refuse; (11) only the comparison cache key (no production caller today) gains the provider axis: the specs / price / reviews L1 and L2 caches carry Claude-extracted values across providers and rollbacks for their TTLs; (12) the engine-unavailable copy implies a transient outage for a stale-client refusal that never resolves (S81-11 renders a reason); (13) `APP_MIN_VERSION` closes the pre-v3 window only with a 1.0.1 binary live (an OTA never changes `nativeApplicationVersion`); (14) the camera compare rebinds its own scope, so a camera request can escalate once in vision and once in the compare; (15) never set any of the seven flags in a local `.env`: the rows set them per test and CI runs them unset; (16) production has no Pillow (it does not join `requirements.in` in this unit, F1-5): a Claude image part above 5,000,000 bytes is REFUSED before dispatch and the request takes the non-delivery; the client transcodes camera uploads to JPEG before upload, so the expected rate is low; the camera upload-size distribution is a canary item of the vision flip, and Pillow (or a client-side cap) is follow-up S81-06b if the refusal count is non-zero; (17) the `/url/compare` consent refusal sits before the credit reserve and before the search-log start, so under `ENABLE_SEARCH_LOG_TRUTH` it writes no failure search-log row (the 401 AUTH_REQUIRED guard writes none either); the text entries' refusal rows ride `text_routes` (F1-11). **Owner steps (names only, in order):** (1) Console: the MYEZ workspace with web search disabled and a spend limit; create `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` on `web` by name only (never on `price-warmer`; never `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL`, `ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN` or `ANTHROPIC_CUSTOM_HEADERS`); (2) after S81-12 and the OTA: `ENABLE_REQUEST_CONTEXT_HEADERS`, `ENABLE_LLM_LEDGER_V2`, `ENABLE_LLM_SPEND_BREAKER` ON in their own windows; (3) the pre-v3 window: `ENABLE_OPENAI_PRE_V3=true` for the window the owner chooses, then unset (and raise `APP_MIN_VERSION` only with 1.0.1 live); (4) one role flag per canary window on fresh never-compared fragrance pairs written into the ledger first; (5) never set a role flag on `price-warmer`. **Canary:** `metadata.llm.by_provider.anthropic` cost and cache_read share, the `[ROUTER]` lines by kind, the `LLM_UNAVAILABLE` 503 count, the refusal and escalation rates, `/health anthropic_breaker.state`, `/health loop_lag_max_60s_ms`, client wall time per compare. **Rollback:** unset the variable (per call, immediate).

**SESSION 81 flag (S81-08b LEGACY-FINGERPRINT-READER, 2026-10-29; default OFF, read PER CALL; a new name, so unset in production by construction (Railway not read); the backend half of the PLAN_S81 client-lane unit S81-08 CLIENT-FIRST-OTA (RULINGS_S81_08 R-S08-4 / R-S08-7 as amended); spec `.qa-s80/S81-08b_UNIT_SPEC.md` of the unit worktree with its adversarial review (BL1-BL2, C1-C14, K1-K5), the rulings `specs/RULINGS_S81_08B.md` (R-S08B-1..R-S08B-11) and the RED gate `specs/GATE_S81_08B.md` (GC-1..GC-11) in `docs/investigations/2026-10-22-session-81-state/`):**
- `ENABLE_LEGACY_FINGERPRINT_READER` (S81-08b, `app/api/auth_routes.py::legacy_fingerprint_reader_enabled`, read per call as the first statement of `_read_valid_legacy_fingerprint`, so OFF reads no header beyond today's; no knob, no credential). **Effect ON:** at POST `/api/v1/auth/register` and in the W3-3 social apply (`_apply_social_device_fingerprint`, reachable only with `ENABLE_SOCIAL_DEVICE_FINGERPRINT` ON, whose production state is UNKNOWN: no dated ledger line names it), a request that carries a valid `X-Device-Fingerprint` AND a valid `X-Device-Fingerprint-Legacy` (64 lower-case hex, full match) that differs from it inherits the device counter through ONE read of `users.device_fingerprint_hash IN (primary, legacy)` (the highest `lifetime_comparisons_used` first, one row) instead of `= primary`; the write is the base write (the caller's own row only) and stores the PRIMARY value, never the legacy one (no upgrade-on-match: no stored row is rewritten, and a social account whose own row already holds a hash keeps it), with max(the own counter at social, the matched counter), so a legacy-hash device carries its counter onto the new-hash row. An invalid, empty or absent legacy header, or one equal to the primary value, is ignored SILENTLY (no log line, never a 400) and the base path runs; a legacy header without a valid primary value matches nothing, as today. One INFO `[LEGACY-FP] site=register|social top=legacy|new|none` per dual read (which value the max-holding row stores; on a tie a log label only), a failure one WARNING `[LEGACY-FP] site=<site> inherit failed: <Type>` (the type name only; the response unchanged); no log line carries a value; no response key, status, header or OpenAPI change. **Why:** after the S81-08 OTA the primary header carries the NEW SecureStore-id hash while every stored row written before it holds the build-3 composition, which the client sends as the legacy header at register and both social sign-ins only (never at share, compares or usage status) until 2027-03-31T21:00Z (the client runtime cutoff; no backend date logic: after it the match degrades to primary-only by header absence; S81-08c removes this reader and the flag); without the reader a post-OTA re-signup on an existing device inherits nothing, silently. Stakes: the per-user daily and monthly free counters are unaffected; the inherited lifetime counter gates only the lifetime-free branch and the `/usage/status` figures (the lifetime figure, and `remaining.daily` / `remaining.monthly`, which `_usage_status_body` reports as the full allowance while on the lifetime-free branch).
- **Flag OFF, or ON without the legacy header (build 3), or ON with an invalid or primary-equal one:** byte-identical (the same DB calls, response bodies and log lines; literal call-list pins REG_BASE / SOCIAL_BASE_NULL / SOCIAL_BASE_OWNED under the full unit configuration, P1-P7 of `tests/test_s81_s08b_legacy_fingerprint.py`; the app-logger record lists on the drop paths and the no-value rule are pinned by `tests/test_s81_s08b_gate_rows.py` (RB8-2), the failure table by RB8-3); the share route, SAME_DEVICE and the referral device lifetime cap are untouched and pinned (P8, P11, P12).
- **Activation (R-S08B-2):** the owner flips `ENABLE_LEGACY_FINGERPRINT_READER=true` on `web` right after this unit deploys, as its OWN step in the PLAN_S81 flip order BEFORE the first OTA; it may precede Apple approval, because ON with no legacy header is byte-identical for build 3; it is independent of, and earlier than, `ENABLE_REQUEST_CONTEXT_HEADERS` (that flag flips after the OTA in its own window; this read is route-level and needs no request context). The OTA precondition is the DATED ledger line of this flip ON ("ENABLE_LEGACY_FINGERPRINT_READER=true on web (deployment <sha>)"), never the merge: an OTA gated only on the merge ships the reader dark, and every post-OTA re-signup then inherits nothing with no error and no log line. Canary after the OTA: keep the pre-OTA email-registered test account (NOT deleted: migration 043's `delete_user_cascade` NULLs `users.device_fingerprint_hash` and zeroes `lifetime_comparisons_used`, so a delete-then-re-register canary reads `top=none` and inherits nothing at base and at HEAD, a false negative) and register a SECOND account on that device with no OS update since the first registration: it logs `[LEGACY-FP] site=register top=legacy` and inherits the first account's lifetime counter. **Rollback:** unset the variable (read per call, immediate).
- **Stated limits:** (1) two request-time sites only (register and the W3-3 social apply; with W3-3 OFF the social half is inert); (2) SAME_DEVICE (`abuse_detection_service.evaluate_invite`) is dead at base (`referral_service._load_invitee` selects no device hash) and stays untouched, stored-vs-stored and out of any header's reach (its revival is the flagged defect row S81-08d); (3) the referral device lifetime cap compares stored values, so legacy-hash and new-hash accounts on one device are two buckets across the transition: at most one extra cap of 3 lifetime invites per device (accepted; S81-09 revisits device identity); (4) after the cutoff a device that never registered inside the window loses its link to its legacy-hash rows; (5) the legacy value is today's composition with the CURRENT osBuildId, so a row stored under an earlier OS build, app id or nonce matches neither header, as at base: the reader restores today's match rate, no more; (6) the header is a client claim: the reader can only raise the inherited counter and never changes what is stored (a client can still escape inheritance with a random primary value, as today); (7) the register except arm keeps its pre-existing `str(exc)` WARNING (flag-independent; the dual function never raises into it); (8) the anon gate keys (`anon:<fp>`) are untouched (build 3 sends no device header on the anonymous routes, and anonymous compares are 401 under `ENABLE_COMPARE_AUTH_REQUIRED`), and CORS `allow_headers` does not list the legacy header (native clients do not preflight; an Expo web build would, like S81-05 limit 5); (9) that the 023 partial index `idx_users_device_fingerprint_active` serves the `IN` read is reasoned from its predicate, not measured on a database; (10) the comm-set base record was re-measured at the rebase main `a8f01379` (247 files; the RED's 241 at `86d5a24d` was the GREEN's base); (11) `users.lifetime_comparisons_used` is nullable (011, INT DEFAULT 0) and PostgreSQL sorts NULL first under `DESC` (postgrest 2.31.0 renders no `nullslast`), so a NULL-counter row would be returned first and read as 0: the base read has the same property within the primary group and the dual read widens it across both; practically unreachable (default 0, 043 writes 0, the 011 RPC COALESCEs); the RED FakeDB sorts NULL as 0; GC-2 binds `desc=True`, so this is a note for S81-09, not a code change.
- **Owner steps (names only; a dated ledger line):** set `ENABLE_LEGACY_FINGERPRINT_READER=true` on `web` after this unit deploys and before the first post-approval OTA, and record the dated ledger line; nothing else (no migration, no credential, no pepper).

**SESSION 81 flags (S81-15 REGISTRY-ROBOTS-DENY, 2026-10-28; both default OFF, read PER CALL; new names, so unset in production by construction (Railway not read); PLAN_S81 unit S81-15 (M4, lane B), owner decisions 7 and 11; spec `.qa-s80/S81-15_UNIT_SPEC.md` of the unit worktree with its adversarial review (B1-B5, C1-C19, K1-K4), the rulings `specs/RULINGS_S81_15.md` (R-S15-1..R-S15-19) and the RED gate `specs/GATE_S81_15.md` (GC-1..GC-13) in `docs/investigations/2026-10-22-session-81-state/`):**
- `ENABLE_MERCHANT_REGISTRY_DB` + knobs `MERCHANT_REGISTRY_MEMO_SECONDS` (600, clamped 300..900), `MERCHANT_REGISTRY_READ_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` (5, clamped 1..30), `MERCHANT_REGISTRY_MAX_STALE_SECONDS` (3600, clamped 900..21600), each read per call, unset / blank / garbage / inf / nan / <= 0 -> the default (S81-15, `app/services/merchant_registry.py::merchant_registry_db_enabled`, a stdlib leaf over `deny_hosts`; the accessors are `source_router.registry_rows()` and `registry_lookup_rows()`). **Effect ON:** the routed registry comes from `kb_merchants` (migration 045, S81-14) through a per-process memo refreshed by ONE paged read (`status in (live, blocked)`, ordered by host, 500-row pages until a short page) dispatched with `asyncio.to_thread` under `asyncio.wait_for` in a background task on the caller's running loop (stale-while-revalidate: a caller never awaits it; a thread with no running loop schedules nothing). TWO sets (ruling R-S15-1): SELECTION (`registry_rows`, read only by `_routable_registry`: the category, mechanism and fan-out selectors, `build_site_discovery_query`, `score_source`, `source_usage`, `is_render_only_domain`) holds the entries of live rows whose `deny` is false on hosts `deny_hosts` does not match; LOOKUP (`registry_lookup_rows`, read by `_registry_row_for_host` and so `registry_tier` / `match_registry_apex` / `descriptor_for_host` / `shopify_pdp_service` and the host-evidence refusals, and by the three direct readers `price_service._curl_timeout_for_url`, `cache_service._registry_domains`, `magento_graphql_service._expected_currency_for`) also keeps blocked and denied rows (`Source.status` "blocked", the new appended `Source.deny`), so a host the robots cron blocks leaves selection but keeps its tier, currency and every global-tier refusal. The snapshot is a sha256 over the decode columns plus the `ENABLE_BH_GCC_CATALOG_SOURCES` state (an equal snapshot keeps the same list objects, so the D03 memo identity holds; an owner SQL edit without an updated_at bump is still seen); catalog-origin entries count only while the catalog flag is ON, literal / operator / discovery entries in both states, and the literal dedup reads every literal-origin entry whatever its status, so a seeded table reproduces today's registry record for record minus fragrantica. Failures (no client, a read error, a timeout, a non-list page, nothing routable) never raise: with no good load the code registry is served; after a good load the last good lists for up to the max-stale window, then the code registry; ONE `[MERCHANT_REGISTRY] refresh failed reason=<type|constant>` WARNING per window. `partner_for_host` returns the stored partner / CTA dict (no caller; never read by scoring). **Flag OFF:** both accessors return `SOURCE_REGISTRY` itself: no read, no task, no line (pinned by `tests/test_s81_s15_registry_robots.py` P1-P9: the 28 p1 cells over D03 x LONGEST_HOST_MATCH x seven falsy spellings, each with a fresh primed memo holding a different DB list). **Preconditions:** 045 applied; the seed run with `--apply` and its counts in the ledger; the robots cron has covered every live host once. **Stated limits:** the first compares of each process use the code registry until the first refresh lands; a table change reaches a process within one window; `structured_comparison_service._bh_locale_known_retail_domains` keeps the code set; the seed's existing-host read is one PostgREST page (it refuses at 1,000 rows).
- `ENABLE_ROBOTS_REGISTRY_CRON` + knobs `ROBOTS_CRON_MAX_HOSTS` (500, clamped 1..5000), `ROBOTS_CHALLENGE_PARK_DAYS` (7, clamped 1..30) (S81-15, `app/services/robots_registry.py`, entry `scripts/cron_robots_registry.py`, a NEW Railway cron service from `railway.robots.json`, never `web` or `price-warmer`, owner-scheduled daily). **Effect ON:** every due host's `/robots.txt` (never read first, then the oldest `robots.next_reread`, then host) is read once a day as QarenBot (`robots_eval.QARENBOT_UA`, now the one UA definition; `sitemap_discovery_service._ROBOTS_UA` is bound to that object), no impersonation, one request at a time with a delay of max(Crawl-delay capped 60 s, 2 s), redirects followed by hand (`allow_redirects=False`, at most 5 hops, the SSRF validator on the first URL and on every hop: a refused first URL is `robots_unreadable` with `health.ssrf` initial, a refused hop `ssrf_redirect`), from the probe ORIGIN (the sample PDP's origin when it is on the row's own site, else `https://<host>` with the sample's origin listed under `robots.other_origins`); fail-closed: 404/410 allow, any other non-2xx, a timeout, an error or a challenge is unreadable, and a challenge is ONLY a wall signature on an HTML-shaped body or `cf-mitigated: challenge` (a CDN 5xx origin outage is unreadable, never parked); the verdict is the most restrictive of the QarenBot and `*` groups over `/`, the sample path and the adapter path; the record is the D14 contract (`state, status, body_sha, fetched_at, next_reread, crawl_delay, origin, source, rules {qarenbot, star}` (verbatim, 200 each) `, probed`; fixture `tests/fixtures/s81_s15_robots_record.json`); live -> blocked `robots_disallow` / `robots_unreadable` / `challenge` / `ssrf_redirect` (with `health.pre_block_status`) and a later allow rejoins (Option A); a candidate is never blocked: it names its first failing gate (robots, tos, pdp, platform) in `health.promotion` and becomes live only when robots allow AND `tos.verdict = allowed` (no PDP fetch before it) AND one validated PDP (`validate_scrape_url`, robots allow on every hop, 2xx, not walled, not an empty shell, a price with a currency from `extract_price_from_html` under the sanitised page title) AND a known platform; a challenge parks the host (`challenge {vendor, parked_until}`); `deny` / `owner` / `tos` blocks and every deny_hosts host are never fetched or written; a residential or operator record younger than 30 days is never replaced by a datacenter read (`health.last_datacenter_result` only, the ruling's Option C rider); one conditional update per host (`eq('updated_at', <seen>)`; a lost race is logged by host and skipped). One INFO per host (host + state only). **Flag OFF:** the entry logs one INFO and returns: no read, no fetch. **The live path is untouched:** no live fetcher reads these verdicts in this unit (D14 LIVE-ROBOTS owns that lookup); with both flags ON the only live effect is that a host the cron blocks leaves SELECTION at the next memo refresh; `search_descriptor.robots_allowed` (2026-08-31) is NOT refreshed by this cron (D14 / S81-16 read `kb_merchants.robots`). **Off-clock seed** `scripts/seed_kb_merchants.py` (dry run by default; `--apply` inserts 410 hosts = 317 live, 92 candidate, 1 blocked deny, 411 entries, each built by `kb_service.build_merchant_row` with `created_at` on INSERT only, and updates existing hosts' seed-owned columns only; `--classify [N]` (default 20) the robots-gated platform probe over live platform-NULL blank-mechanism rows, literal rows included; `--source residential --hosts ...` records a residential robots read). **Stated limits:** the first pass needs one daily run per 500 hosts; one origin per host row (a cross-origin sample or adapter origin is listed, not read: D14 treats it as missing); a rule list past 200 lines is truncated and marked `rules_truncated`; the three 2026-08-31 residential reads (klinq.com, nazih.qa, numberc.com) are seeded with that date, so they are past 30 days on the first run and need a fresh `--source residential` read first, else the datacenter result applies; live adapters still impersonate Chrome until the owner's UA ruling after M6; Crawl-delay is parsed in-house; the cron runs the code of its last deploy (watch patterns).
- **Owner steps (names only; a dated ledger line per step):** (1) apply 045 (S81-14) with its PRECHECK / POSTCHECK; (2) `python -m scripts.seed_kb_merchants` (dry run) and record the count line, then `--apply` with the service-role variable NAMES `SUPABASE_URL`, `SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY`; (3) from a residential connection, `--source residential --hosts klinq.com,nazih.qa,numberc.com --apply`; (4) optionally `--classify` (20, dry run) and record the counts; (5) create the Railway cron service from `railway.robots.json` (start `python -m scripts.cron_robots_registry`, restart NEVER), schedule it daily, set `ENABLE_ROBOTS_REGISTRY_CRON=true`, `SUPABASE_URL` and `SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY` on that service only; (6) review ToS for every candidate reported with `failing_gate = tos` (`tos {reviewed_at, verdict, terms_url}`); (7) after one full pass covers every live host and after App Review approval, `ENABLE_MERCHANT_REGISTRY_DB=true` on `web` in its own canary window (watch the `[MERCHANT_REGISTRY]` lines and the per-category source counts); rollback = unset the variable (per call, immediate).

## Tests

```bash
# Free unit tests (~$0)
python -m pytest tests/ -v -m "not (live_unit or live_db or integration)" --ignore=tests/test_integration.py

# Live unit tests (iHerb, Serper, GPT — ~$0.03)
LIVE=1 python -m pytest tests/ -v -m "not (live_db or integration)"

# Integration tests (live Railway — ~$0.06)
LIVE=1 python -m pytest tests/test_integration.py -v -m integration

# Full suite
LIVE=1 python -m pytest tests/ -v --timeout=180
```

- `python -m py_compile <file>` for syntax checks; `node node_modules/typescript/bin/tsc --noEmit` (from `SmartCompareApp/`) for frontend types.
- `conftest.py` auto-loads `.env` via python-dotenv, then STRIPS the
  credential-bearing names from it (issue #48) so the default tier runs
  credential-free, same as CI. **`LIVE=1` is what restores them** — the
  `live_unit` / `live_db` / `integration` markers no longer opt in on their
  own, so `-m live_db` without `LIVE=1` skips every selected test instead of
  running it. PowerShell: `$env:LIVE=1; python -m pytest ...`. See
  `tests/_env_safety.py`.
- ~557 test files (`test_<feature>.py`, roughly one per service; ~15,900 free-tier nodes collected — M13-124 corrected the stale "~100" figure 2026-09-01). 80%+ coverage target for new features.
- No regressions: all existing tests must pass before merging.
- **Eval gate (Bundle B B.6):** pre-merge `python -m scripts.eval_runner --subset smoke20 --mode regression --baseline-run-id 54b603e8-4eab-41c9-a34d-a5e391446559 --concurrency 1` — `54b603e8-…` is the ONE baseline run id the gates must pass: the newest smoke20 anchor (next bullet), which superseded the earlier smoke20 anchor and, before it, the Bundle B S1 full-200 baseline (21.0%), whose subset never matched smoke20 (`docs/runbooks/qaren-eval.md`). Measurement runs ALWAYS `--concurrency 1` (walls are load-sensitive); full-200 needs `--allow-full` + dispatcher GO (~600-1,000 Serper credits). Runbooks: `docs/runbooks/qaren-eval.md` + `qaren-gold-set.md`.
- **Eval anchor (hoisted 2026-08-30 out of the archived 2026-06-17 faithful-results bullet; this is the ONE intentional duplicate - the source bullet is verbatim in `docs/SESSION_BUNDLES.md`):** **Eval anchor = the FULL UUID `54b603e8-4eab-41c9-a34d-a5e391446559` (smoke20 winner 0.50). Gates MUST pass the full UUID (`--baseline-run-id 54b603e8-4eab-41c9-a34d-a5e391446559`) — the short `54b603e8` 400s `invalid input syntax for type uuid` → a FALSE "GATE FAIL" (NOT a regression). COLD smoke20 shows `pass_rate=0`/`price=0` BY DESIGN (the baseline row itself does too — 30s cap) → judge by the axis AVGS (winner/specs/factual), never pass_rate. `python -m scripts.eval_runner` does NOT auto-load `.env` (only pytest's conftest does) → `set -a; source .env; set +a` (or `load_dotenv(override=True)`) first, else the baseline fetch fails "SUPABASE_URL must be set". free-unit baseline RE-CAPTURED to **14** real nodes (M13-17, 2026-09-01) then CLEANED to **11** (M21-W3 `eb331cb`, 2026-09-02 - 3 rows individually proven green and removed, the order-dependent test_database_service row fixed as a TEST bug; mirror regenerated — `tests/.pre_impl_failures.txt`; the old "48" was a stale, credential-captured snapshot padded with 35 xfail'd value_math nodes, so it reported false regressions on a clean credential-free clone) then pruned to **8** (B3, session 80, 2026-10-20: the three `test_camera_vision.py` mocked rows, fixed in the test and proven green, mirror regenerated) + the two `NETWORK_FLAKY_EXCLUDE` GET-hangers the gate drops on both sides.**
- **⚠️ CI-GREEN IS NOT ALL-TESTS-PASS — do not quote it as if it were.** `backend-tests` is green because the "Run unit tests" step builds `$DESELECT` from the committed `tests/.pre_impl_failures.txt` and **skips those 8 nodes**. They are RED on a plain credential-free run (two of them, `test_extraction_prompt_bundle_c` and `test_supplement_branch_genuine`, were observed passing on the session-80 dev box at 81df2b64 and stay listed until proven on CI, #89). The three `tests/test_camera_vision.py::TestIdentifyProductsMocked` nodes that used to be among them (a MagicMock usage fixture: `TypeError` at `openai_service.py:81`, the `cached_tokens > 0` line; a fixture defect, not a product bug) were fixed in the test by B3 (session 80, 2026-10-20: a `types.SimpleNamespace` usage) and run in CI again. The deselect is a REGRESSION gate — it stops NEW breakage — and it is doing that job correctly; it is not evidence the suite passes. Fixing the remaining 8 is **`#89`**, still open. When reporting CI status, say "green with 8 known nodes deselected" (the 8 node-id rows of `tests/.pre_impl_failures.txt`, recounted 2026-10-20 by B3), never "0 failed".
- **Known RED-by-design:** `tests/test_value_math.py` (**35** TDD stubs for unimplemented Bundle C v1.1 value-math A.6.x fns — recounted 2026-08-25, the old "24" was stale) — not a regression. Contained by a module-level `pytest.mark.xfail(strict=False)` since #49, so they no longer redden the build; `ci.yml` runs with `-rxX` so the day they XPASS the marker gets removed. Gate batches must exclude network-dependent "free" tests (e.g. `test_rate_limiting_complete.py` does a real GET).
- **The dev machine can run a DIFFERENT fastapi/starlette than `requirements.txt` pins** (observed 2026-09-01: local `fastapi 0.115.0` / `starlette 0.38.6` vs lock `fastapi==0.141.1` / `starlette==1.6.0` — the lock is what CI and Railway install), so a framework-introspection test can pass locally and fail only in CI. **Framework introspection must therefore be DUCK-TYPED, never shape-assuming:** on 0.141 an `app.routes` entry can be an `_IncludedRouter` with no `.path` (naive `r.path` raises `AttributeError`; the getattr-defended `getattr(r, "path", "")` silently sees ZERO routes and reports a FALSE "not mounted"). Shared walker + positive control: **`tests/_route_introspection.py`** (M19) — use it, do not re-flatten to `for r in app.routes`. Re-sync a stale local env with `pip install -r requirements.txt -r requirements-dev.txt`.
- **Windows codec trap:** always pass `encoding='utf-8'` to `subprocess.run`/`open`/`read_text` — `text=True` alone decodes cp1252 and manufactures mojibake from clean UTF-8 (bit 3 independent tools on 2026-06-10). Byte-compare BOTH sides before reporting any non-ASCII diff.

```bash
cd SmartCompareApp && npm audit --audit-level=high
```

## Pre-launch (App Store / Google Play)

ToS/Privacy fact base, code-side blockers (delete cascade, expo-notifications plugin, Sentry URL scrubbing, ToS clickwrap + 13+ gate, `ai_sharing_enabled` default), stale legal docs, and the 25-item DECISIONS REQUIRED block: see `docs/plans/2026-05-06-tos-fact-base.md` and `docs/plans/2026-05-06-tos-evidence/`. Stale legal markdown at `app/legal/{privacy_policy,terms_of_service}.md` is served live and is still the DRAFT template of 2026-03-26 that says "Qaren" (`privacy@qaren.app` / `legal@qaren.app`) — the app is MYEZ since session 69 — and promises an AI-sharing opt-out (`privacy_policy.md` §"Your right to opt out") that routes nothing (#266); the #274 AI-consent sheet links it, so U8 (8 legal inputs) is a launch blocker — REPLACE, do not patch.

**Age policy (locked):** 13+ general audience including teens. Apple **13+** (the 12+ tier no longer exists — session 69 LL-8; the recommended handling — answer the questionnaire honestly, Override to Higher Age Rating → 13+, Age Assurance = No — is decision D9, still pending with Ahmed), Google Play **Teen**. Do NOT enroll in Apple "Kids" or Google "Designed for Families".

**Canary phasing:** With <10 testers pre-launch, set new-feature canary % to 100 — lower % statistically hash-buckets a small tester set out of the feature being tested. Drop to 10 only at App Store soft-launch, then ramp 10→50→100 per `docs/runbooks/qaren-canary-onboarding.md`.

- `ENABLE_BLOCKLIST_PRECISION_V2` (W4-8 Half B, PO-CATEGORIES-I18N-03/-09, `app/services/content_safety_service.py::blocklist_precision_v2_enabled`, **MERGED #227**) — **Effect ON:** L1 `check_query_intent`, L2 `is_text_safe` and `filter_shopping_items` use the v2 matcher: the v2 category lists in `app/data/content_blocklist.json` (version 2) are the v1 lists VERBATIM — nothing dropped, nothing added to the blocking side, a safety list never gets more permissive on an unlisted phrasing (ruling R11) — and precision comes from a per-token EXEMPTION map (`v2.exempt.<cat>.<lang>.<token>` = qualifiers): a query matched by an exempted token is not blocked by THAT token when it also carries one of its qualifiers (whole token, casefolded, `re.escape`'d); every other token still blocks. Qualifiers = the measured legitimate collisions plus the named context words: EN `silencer` → exhaust / generator / bosch / walker / hilux / car / muffler; EN `tactical knife` → gerber (+ the measured EDC brands); AR opium (hamza spelling) → perfume, perfumes, San Laurent, Black, ysl, yves saint laurent, saint laurent; the AR rifle / silencer / combat-knife tokens → the measured water / air / Nerf, exhaust / generator, Gerber forms. **Self-contained; written by the second session, which owns the W3 remainder while the session above owns W3-2/W1-5/W1-6 and all of W4.** Full state, per-unit, in `docs/investigations/2026-09-11-w3-remainder-state.md` — read that before resuming any W3 unit. Ten units (W3-3, W3-4, W3-6, W3-7, W3-9, W3-11b/c/d, W3-13, W3-14, W3-15, W3-16) have MEASURED specs at `<worktree>/.qa-w3b/<KEY>_UNIT_SPEC.md`, each adversarially reviewed and **APPROVED_WITH_RULINGS** (the rulings section at the end of each file is BINDING and supersedes the body). Six carry partial red/green work saved on `wip/s65d-<unit>-partial` (spec force-added there); their worktrees are still dirty with the same work, so a resume needs no restore — and **never** `git checkout` to restore in them.

**Four measured facts that change the wave plan.** (1) **W3-9 is already fixed on main** — B1 (`e3d9adb5`, in #138) rewrites every `lucide-react-native` barrel import to a per-icon file at BUILD time (3,832 → 2,192 modules, 68 icon files), so the 35 barrel imports are the INTENDED source form and the plan's "ban the barrel with eslint" test would be wrong at HEAD; the unit shrinks to optional test-only fence hardening (`require()`, dynamic `import()`, `import =` and `.js`/`.jsx` sources are unfenced, and any of them silently re-adds all 1,703 icon modules with CI green). Phones still run the PRE-B1 bundle until `eas update --branch preview --clear-cache`. (2) **`@react-navigation/*` ships ESM only (no `lib/commonjs`), so its real router has NEVER executed under jest** — every suite that touched the package mocked it; testing real deep-link resolution needs a narrow `babel-jest` transform for `node_modules/@react-navigation/**/*.js`, a shared-config change whose proof is the full-suite comparison (baseline at `ed75dc70`: tsc 5.9.3 clean, jest 281 suites passed / 3 skipped, 2,752 tests, 44 snapshots). (3) **Removing `RECORD_AUDIO` from `app.json` was behaviourally INERT** (measured 2026-09-11 by running the installed expo-camera/expo-image-picker plugins), as was `recordAudioAndroid: false` — at that time the microphone opt-out did the work and the `app.json` line was a source-shape pin only. Since #254 (session 69) the opt-out is gone (`microphonePermission: false` removed the purpose string while expo-camera still links audio APIs — ITMS-90683 risk, EXPO-07): `expo-camera` and `expo-image-picker` carry the honest string "MYEZ only uses the camera to photograph products. Audio is never recorded.", and `android.blockedPermissions: [RECORD_AUDIO]` is the pinned Android guard — native, so it needs the production `eas build`. (4) **`{"ai_sharing_enabled": "yes"}` returns 200 `True`, not 422**, on the installed pydantic 2.7.0 through this repo's own handlers.

**Process rule earned here: never let a spec reach a red phase unreviewed.** Ten adversarial spec reviewers refuted 5–13 measured claims each in specs written with the same care, including four where the campaign plan's own red test would have been wrong at HEAD. Every spec must carry its base SHA and every review must re-anchor it — with two sessions merging all day, anchors went stale within hours.

**Ahmed, new in this lane:** add an `EXPO_TOKEN` repo secret (W3-13's channel-freshness job skips cleanly without it, and the `preview` channel is 33 first-parent commits behind main); `railway login` — the MCP token expired, so prod flag state is currently INFERRED, not read; W3-16 needs migration **038** (this lane owns 038, the other owns 039+) and an age-gate shape decision; W3-7 needs icon artwork before its icon test can be anything but `test.todo`; W3-10 still waits on the retailer-link product call.

It now asserts on the envelope's `code`/`error` fields. **New standing rules from this batch:** every numeric env knob parsed with `float()` must reject non-finite values; a byte-identity construction pin asserts values, not argument names; abandon-vs-cancel and callback-ordering claims need their own pins (a synchronous `not task.done()` check cannot distinguish them); pin `.strip().lower()` on every flag reader with an upper-case or padded row; and assert on envelope FIELDS, never on a substring of a body that carries a random id. **Follow-up sweep recorded, not fixed:** nine other reader sites parse an env knob with bare `float()` and accept `inf`/`nan` — `SUPABASE_POSTGREST_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` (`database_service.py`, where `inf` would reinstate the unbounded client W0-2 exists to bound), `PRICE_RACE_TIMEOUT`, `REAL_ACTION_MIN_SECONDS`, the LLM-breaker snapshot TTL, `CLASSIFY_LLM_TIMEOUT`, `MAX_MONTHLY_COST` (where `nan` makes every budget comparison False), `WINNER_DIM_GAP_TOLERANCE`, `WINNER_PRICE_AUTHORITY_POINTS` and `WINNER_VALUE_WEIGHT_SCALE`.

**SCRAPER TOOLING — MEASURED bake-off 2026-08-25 (not desk research).** 9 real registry targets, every
contender judged by the repo's OWN `extract_price_from_html`. Scoreboard (priced/9): **curl_cffi 4,
Scrape.do 4, crawl4ai default 4, crawl4ai+patchright 4, Firecrawl 0, Zyte 0 (account suspended).**
Full write-up: `docs/investigations/2026-08-25-scraper-bakeoff.md`.
- **`curl_cffi` READ sephora.me** — 200, zero `_abck`, JSON-LD **77.000 BHD**, 1.39s, free: the exact
  price Zyte was procured for, while plain Playwright AND crawl4ai AND crawl4ai+patchright all got 403.
  **ONE sample — not actionable.** #93 specifies 20+ samples over 24h from Railway egress before
  retiring the Zyte tier. Do not rewire sephora on the strength of one lucky fetch.
- **Firecrawl's 0/9 is OUR bug (#92, P1):** `firecrawl_service.py` hardcodes `formats:["html"]` =
  Firecrawl's CLEANED html, which returns **0 script tags / 0 ld+json**. The extractor reads ld+json,
  so the integration **cannot return a price on any page, ever**, while billing 1 credit each. With
  `formats:["rawHtml"]` the same URL yields 1.400 BHD and is 5.4x faster. Firecrawl also 500s on both
  controls curl cracks free, and returns **HTTP 200 + bills on a real 404**.
- **crawl4ai — rejected on measurement.** Its patchright "undetected" mode was **byte-identical to
  default on all 9 targets** and contributed zero, including both walled ones. It beat plain
  Playwright on 1 of 9, purely via its default User-Agent — and crawl4ai under-rendered that page
  into a confident-wrong "6.02 BHD" where bare Playwright returned the correct 16.0.
- **Scrape.do — narrow keeper**, behind a "curl returned a WAF block" trigger only. Its single real
  win was eros.ae (Link11 491, blocks curl+Playwright+crawl4ai). The other 3 duplicated free curl
  results at 9-50x latency, two of them EXCEEDING the 15s live clock.
- **Registry is wrong on 4 of 9 rows (#94):** `matalanme` + `xcite` are flagged `render-only` but
  plain curl reads them in <1s, so they have been escalating to PAID render for free pages; `bfab`'s
  sample_url is a category page; `letoile`'s is a 404. The registry needs a cleanup pass more than
  the stack needs a vendor.


A full audit of `main c630436` produced **GitHub issues #46–#81** (12 P1, 23 P2, 1 P3), each self-contained with verified `file:line`. Three are implemented and pushed, **no PRs opened, nothing merged, nothing deployed**: `fix/46-dependency-lock` (`d6c52ab`), `fix/58-model-config` (`b9e962e`), `fix/59-subtype-specs` (`48daac6`). Each passed the repo comm gate against `origin/main` with **branch-only-NEW == []**.

**Three findings that change how you work in this repo — full detail in `docs/investigations/2026-08-24-audit-implementation-handoff.md`:**

1. **CI has been RED on main since at least 2026-07-07** (176 failed / 9,359 passed on `c630436`). Causes: a missing `pillow` dev dependency (fixed on `fix/46`) and the RED-by-design `tests/test_value_math.py` stubs that CI never excluded (still open — **#49**). "Tests green" has not been a real merge gate; do not treat a green local run as proof until #49 lands.
2. **`pytest tests/` hangs — the default suite makes LIVE network calls.** `magento_graphql_service.py:275` and `noon_service.py:170` fetch real retailer sites in executor threads that the pytest timeout cannot kill (this is **#70** reproducing in-suite), and with `.env` present Serper 403s and OpenAI 429s stall the retries (**#48**). Run targeted file sets, excluding adapter/network-heavy files.
3. **`app/config.py` is a trap, not merely dead.** It declares seven REQUIRED pydantic fields and instantiates `Settings()` at import (`:55`), so importing it raises `ValidationError` wherever those env vars are absent. Never put new config there — `app/services/model_config.py` is the pattern to copy (env reads, safe defaults, zero required credentials). Related: `openai_service.py:20` builds `AsyncOpenAI` at module import, and every current openai release rejects keyless construction, so `import app.main` fails without `OPENAI_API_KEY` (verified across 2.54.0/3.2.0/3.3.1 — pre-existing, not a pinning artifact).

**Model ids are now config, not code** (#58): `OPENAI_MODEL_{VERDICT,STANDARD,VISION,CRITIC,MODERATION}`, resolved per call, logged once at startup as `[models] …`. Defaults are unchanged (`gpt-4o` / `gpt-4o-mini`). **Do NOT flip to a GPT-5 id without a live smoke test** — GPT-5 rejects `temperature=0` outright (the verdict's determinism A/B depends on it), requires `max_completion_tokens`, and counts invisible reasoning tokens against that same budget so a straight `1000` carry-over can return empty content. The shims (`sampling_kwargs`, `token_limit_kwargs`) prevent the 400s; they cannot restore determinism.

- **Follow-up units (recorded, not blocking):** faces.ae-class visible-text currency rung for `_page_currency_evidence` (own flag + corpus before/after); `classify_capture` "access denied" false-positive on om.swissarabian.com (needs title/heading anchoring, 1/414); scentsplit decants price = AHMED product decision; multiplicity discriminator (VariantDescriptor); sqlfluff config pass. Serper 403 + OpenAI 429 were still dead then (user chose scraper-variety over key rotation at the time) (superseded: see the SESSION 71 block); Railway-egress re-measure precondition stands.

## Fragrance/global scrape-validation findings (2026-08-25/26) -- STILL LOAD-BEARING; branch narrative archived

**Branch narrative ARCHIVED 2026-08-30.** The `feature/fragrance-hybrid-capture` branch state, its flag-OFF byte-identity proof, and the P0 currency-mismatch write-up (`price_service.py:8818`; 21 of 92 Gulf pages lost their price, 6 shipped an inflated list price) were moved VERBATIM into `docs/SESSION_BUNDLES.md`, section "2026-08-25/26 fragrance data-provenance branch (archived from CLAUDE.md 2026-08-30)". That wave is MERGED (`6205a7c`) and its live state is the block immediately above. Everything below stays here because it is still load-bearing.

**THE FINDING THAT REFRAMES THE PRODUCT: only PRICE is scraped.** `extract_specs`
(`extraction_service.py:1147`) and `extract_reviews` (`:1356`) are OpenAI calls whose only substantive
input is a Serper snippet digest (`organic[:5]`, truncated to 3000/2500 chars,
`structured_comparison_service.py:6945`). All 13 adapter/renderer modules contain `specs` ZERO times and
`aggregateRating|reviewCount|ratingValue` ZERO times; `reviewBody` appears ZERO times in `app/`. The
prompt at `extraction_service.py:515-516` **orders** the model to fall back to training data, and the
output is cached 7 days. Reviews are properly guarded and go EMPTY instead; only SPECS are fabricated.
`derive_rating_from_scores` (`response_builder.py:160-163`) ships a **constant 3.6** rating when there
is no data at all.

**GLOBAL VALIDATION (328 pages / 163 hosts / 26 countries) KILLED THREE ASSUMPTIONS:**
1. The GCC currency rule ("dot=decimal, comma=thousands") scores **1% in EU-South**, 56% in DACH,
   65.6% corpus-wide, and every failure is a silent 100x-1000x. **Locale does not fix it** (douglas.ch
   prints the product price with a dot and the shipping banner with a comma in one document). The rule
   that works is structural last-separator **+ the ISO-4217 minor unit**: 371/371 = **100%**, all six
   regions. But the real answer is architectural: **take the price from JSON-LD, always** — 353 of 360
   JSON-LD values are already machine-normalised with zero comma-decimals, while OG is 11% deviant and
   must never be `float()`ed directly.
2. **Parsing was never the hard part — SELECTION is.** Even at 100% parse accuracy the first
   price-shaped number on a page is right only **41%** of the time (UK **13%**). Median price-shaped
   tokens per PDP: Gulf 2, UK 6, US 8, max 17. The decoys are legally mandated (Grundpreis, per-litre
   unit price, EU Omnibus 30-day-lowest, BNPL). `notino.co.uk` renders the per-litre price under the
   **same `data-testid`** as the product price, 8 characters apart, at exactly 10x.
3. **`product:sale_price:amount` is GULF-ONLY** — 7 occurrences in 328 pages, all 7 Salla/Zid, ZERO
   across 206 non-Gulf pages. The rule must be scoped to those platforms, not left generic.

Also: `detect_platform` returns **"unknown" on 43%** of usable pages and `nextjs` is not a platform (it
fires across five backends; `sephora.me` is SFCC underneath). The extractor returns **no price on 14 of
28** large international retailers (`ProductGroup`+`hasVariant` 9, `@graph`-wrapped 4, empty `offers` 1).
Ratings double outside Bahrain (38% vs 16%) but review **bodies do not** (12% vs 8.7%) because 50% of
pages load a widget and 85% of those ship zero text server-side. **GDPR/CMP walls gate 0 of 247 pages**
on raw HTTP, and `valueAddedTaxIncluded` appears on 2 of 247 — do not build a VAT branch.

**⛔ DO NOT PROPOSE SCRAPING FRAGRANTICA OR PARFUMO.** Both `robots.txt` disallow Claude agents by name —
Fragrantica names `ClaudeBot`, `Claude-SearchBot` **and `Claude-User`** (user-initiated fetches), each
`Disallow: /`. Reading only the `User-agent: *` group sees `ai-input=yes` and reaches the wrong
conclusion. Both then actively defended against our crawling mid-session: Fragrantica went 12/12 to
`403 cf-mitigated`, and **Parfumo began serving decoy pages** — correct `<title>`, but the notes,
accords, year, longevity and perfumer of a different perfume, with invented note names. 35/35
mismatched. **Any Parfumo-derived figure after 2026-08-25 23:07 is fiction.** A fragrance spec database
needs a licensing conversation or a commercial provider.

**SCRAPER ROSTER — no new vendor key is needed.** Free `curl_cffi impersonate=chrome` prices **84%** of
the 94 live Gulf fragrance sources with ZERO genuine WAF walls, and 75% of rows globally. Rejected on
measurement: playwright (7/12 vs free curl's 11/12; its 403s are self-inflicted by the HeadlessChrome
UA), **patchright (byte-for-byte identical to plain playwright on 12/12)**, primp (zero adds on 12/12),
**Firecrawl `json`/LLM mode (FABRICATES — returned review bodies occurring 0 times in the page and
`launch_year "2026"`)**, vendor geo (our egress IS Bahrain; `geoCode=bh` costs 10 credits for identical
bytes), markdown output (drops JSON-LD to 0 on every vendor), Common Crawl (1/12), Bing/DDG, Wikidata
(0/8 for notes), judge.me API (per-merchant token, unbuyable), YouTube (`search.list` = 100 units ->
~49 comparisons/day). **Size-aware Shopify variants are dead: 0 of 999 fragrance products have two
distinct ml variants** — size lives in `tags` and `body_html`, which the adapter already receives.

### ⛔ THE ZERO-REGRESSION GATE IS MODULE-REFERENCE-BASED. NEVER FILENAME-KEYWORD-BASED.

**Selecting the gate's test files by FILENAME KEYWORD would have shipped this wave green with a
deterministic regression in it.** Matching test filenames against the changed modules' names yielded
**54 files and an EMPTY branch-only failure set**. The real regression —
`tests/test_correctness_coverage_review_findings.py::test_H_jsonld_flag_off_carries_name_not_brand`,
which passes at `8adaefb` and fails at `02d5d33` in the SHIPPED default configuration, 2/2
deterministic — sat in a file whose NAME references nothing that changed. It was only reachable via the
**93 files that grep-REFERENCE the changed modules** (`price_service`, `shopify_pdp_service`,
`platform_router`).

So: **build the gate's file set by grepping the test tree for imports/references to every module the
branch touches, then run that set at BASE and at HEAD and `comm` the failures.** A filename-keyword
set is not a subset shortcut — it is a different, much smaller, and systematically wrong set. This is
now a standing rule, not a one-off observation: the miss is confirmed, and the test it missed is the
one this wave had to adjudicate (see the `H — THE JSON-LD CANDIDATE DICT UNDER ROLLBACK` block in that
file).

**MEASUREMENT TRAPS — these produced wrong conclusions in this very session:**
- **An empty `product_name` makes the JSON-LD branch unreachable** (`brand=""` -> every Product dropped
  at `price_service.py:8757`). A sweep passing `""` manufactures a fake failure cohort and understates
  free-curl capture as 72% when it is 84%.
- **`ENABLE_EXACT_PRICE_GATE=true` masks extraction bugs** — the gate rejects most cached pages so
  everything returns `None`. Use `false` to isolate extraction, `true` for shipped behaviour, and always
  say which.
- **Substring block-detection is worthless**: `<script id="captcha-bootstrap">` ships on every Shopify
  page. It fired on 80 of 94 pages with ZERO correct fires. Order any verdict ladder capture-FIRST.
- Ratio tests cannot tell a sale price from a list price after conversion — pin exact expected values.

**Zero-network corpora for any future work** (git-excluded, in the `sc-scraper-proof` worktree):
`_proof/html/` 92 Gulf PDPs + `_proof/sweep2_curl_cffi.jsonl`; `_proof/global/corpus.json` +
`_proof/global/html/` 429 global pages. ⚠️ **`_proof/sweep2.py` is NOT zero-network and is NOT a gate** (corrected 2026-09-07, SESSION 65 W0):
`python _proof/sweep2.py curl_cffi 6 BHD` re-fetches all 94 retailer sites LIVE at `:105` and
OVERWRITES `_proof/html/` at `:110` (the docstring's "cached so variants re-run without re-fetching"
describes the cache being POPULATED, not consulted). Two runs on an IDENTICAL tree three minutes
apart disagreed on 9 of 94 rows in BOTH directions, so its digest can never express code equality —
and because `scripts/verify_flag_byte_identity.py` reads that same `_proof/html/`, running the sweep
INVALIDATES a recorded byte-identity base. Use the canonical tool for flag-OFF equality; treat the
sweep as a live-capture measurement only, and re-record the base afterwards.

**EVERY NUMBER ABOVE CAME FROM ONE BAHRAIN RESIDENTIAL IP.** Railway egress is a datacenter ASN
elsewhere. Re-measure host reachability and Shopify-Markets currency behaviour (it 302'd `/en-om` and
`/en-ae` to `/en-bh` for us) FROM RAILWAY before trusting a capacity number or retiring a paid fallback.

**Five more gate rules (2026-09-07/08, each from a miss that reached CI or main):**
- **The file set is the UNION of the plain rule-grep and any hand-added importers, run at HEAD WITH the unit's own NEW test files.** Session 65's W0-3 test drove `cache_service`'s import-time init with `importlib.reload` on the real module, rebinding every function while `from … import delete_cached` importers kept the originals — `test_negcache_genuine_invalidation::test_module_imports_delete_cached` failed only in full-suite order, invisible to a per-unit set that excluded the unit's own file and missed five files that NAME the module directly (including the victim; one of them reaches it only through `patch("app.services.cache_service.…")` strings) — that hand-curated 47-file set added importers by judgement instead of taking the union with the plain rule-grep. Fix pattern for import-time tests: a PRIVATE module instance via `spec_from_file_location` + `module_from_spec` with the real dotted name (caplog still works), never `sys.modules`.
- **Backend tests that SCAN `SmartCompareApp/` belong in the mobile comm set even when no backend file changed.** `tests/test_events_allowlist_superset.py` greps the client for `trackEvent('…')` literals and asserts `feedback_routes.VALID_EVENT_TYPES` is a superset; M23's A4 fired `compare_cancelled`, the local gates (module-reference on `auth_routes.py`) could not see it, CI did (`0e645ea1`). Add `grep -rl "SmartCompareApp" tests/` to every mobile gate set.
- **The byte-identity control is a post-head BASE re-run.** `_proof/html/` is live-overwritable (`_proof/sweep2.py` refetches 94 sites), so a base/head pair proves nothing about the corpus by itself; run base, then head, then base AGAIN and require the second base run to match head RECORD-BY-RECORD over the payload's `results` array — not by the OVERALL digest, nor by the whole payload JSON (which is exactly what that digest hashes), because both also carry `flags_forced_off`. #136's gate: `ec120b8b…`, payloads 795,636 B identical, non_none 829 on both days.
- **A mutation check belongs in the GATE, not only in review.** In Session 65's W1 batch three tests across two units named a requirement without pinning it: deleting the entire shutdown handler, deleting the line that feeds the loop-lag metric, and deleting the whole adapter TIMEOUT branch each left its suite green — the last because since CPython 3.11 `asyncio.TimeoutError IS TimeoutError`, so an assertion on `"TIMEOUT" in msg.upper()` matched the ERROR line too. Rule: for every unit, delete the fix and confirm its test reddens before the commit; a test that survives its own fix's removal is decoration.
- **Local-vs-lock drift is not only a fastapi story.** Same batch, three instances: `sentry-sdk` pinned 2.68.1 / installed 2.54.0, `uvicorn` pinned 0.52.4 / installed 0.30.0 (boot-critical: a renamed flag there is a crash-loop, not a lost feature), and a spec asserting a measurement taken on the installed version as if pinned. Where installing the pin is not allowed, write the assertion so it runs on whatever version is present and let CI settle it on the pinned build; say in the report which version the local number came from.
- **Every backend comm set includes the SOURCE-SCANNING pins (session 75, PR #341):** the tests that glob or read `app/` (or the start command) as source -- `tests/test_model_config.py` (the stray-model-literal pin), `test_model_config_enforced.py`, `test_prompt_fence.py`, `test_u3c_store_false_pin.py`, `test_start_command*.py`, `test_migration_index_predicate_immutability.py`, `test_openai_key_log_hygiene_s69.py`, `test_sentry_channels_u8d_unit.py` -- reference no changed module by name, so a set chosen by grep-reference can never pick them: COST-METER's 151-file set, two adversaries and a final adversary were green and CI went red on ONE node after 14 minutes (the five exact-key model ids of the new price table). Union the set with those files at HEAD; an allowlist entry for a DATA table (not a dispatch site) is fine with the reason in the comment (ruling X15). The twin of the mobile rule for tests that scan `SmartCompareApp/`.
